## 2026-06-18: Tunnel Duplicate Name, Layout, Delete Verification, and GatewayPorts Restart Fix

Current baseline before this stage:
`30728d3516acc52be37c30401ea3fb30ed4b0543`.

Stage: fix real-user tunnel issues around duplicate profile names, profile card
density, edit/list placement, GatewayPorts restart semantics, and deletion port
release verification.

Windows executable: `build/bin/ServerPilot.exe`

Windows executable SHA-256:
`D5FBF33CF3F5D6EE858B3F3A9043F5A6407B44BCB21001AA7774E790619D74FE`

### User Feedback Addressed

1. Saving a duplicate tunnel profile name showed raw SQLite error:
   `idx_tunnel_profiles_server_name` and `(2067)`.
2. Created profile cards were too tall; stop/delete actions should be compact
   and top-right.
3. Edit configuration and created profile list needed to swap positions.
4. GatewayPorts helper changed `sshd_config`, but old remote-forward listeners
   stayed loopback-only because the tunnel was not recreated.
5. Deleting a tunnel profile could leave the port reachable.

### Scope

- Changed tunnel profile persistence, tunnel manager lifecycle, tunnel Wails API,
  tunnel UI/store/types/tests, and generated Wails bindings.
- Did not modify SSH runtime.
- Did not modify Terminal PTY lifecycle.
- Did not modify Monitor runtime.
- Did not modify SFTP/SCP runtime.
- Did not modify Local Terminal runtime.
- Did not modify `DisconnectServer(serverID)` semantics.
- Did not modify workspace state model.
- Did not modify SecretStore / Key Vault.
- Did not modify Backup / Import / Export.
- Tunnel runtime remains independent from terminal/SFTP/monitor sessions.

### Root Causes

- Tunnel profile save relied on SQLite to enforce the same-server unique name
  constraint and did not translate the database error into a typed user error.
- `StopProfile` did not return release-verification failure to callers, so
  deletion could proceed even when the user could still connect to the port.
- A running OpenSSH remote-forward listener keeps its original bind address.
  Reloading `sshd` after changing GatewayPorts does not convert that existing
  listener from loopback-only to public.

### Implemented

- Added typed duplicate-name error:
  `TUNNEL_PROFILE_NAME_EXISTS: 该服务器下已存在同名端口转发配置，请修改名称。`
- `SaveTunnelProfile` now preflights same-server name conflicts and excludes the
  current profile ID on update.
- Raw SQLite unique constraint fallback maps to the same Chinese error.
- Added tests for:
  - duplicate create;
  - same name on different server;
  - unchanged-name update;
  - renaming to another same-server profile name.
- `StopTunnel` and `StopProfile` now return errors.
- Worker stop closes listener, active connections, accept loop context, waits
  for worker exit, and verifies port release.
- Local/dynamic release verification uses local TCP connect failure.
- Remote release verification uses the existing tunnel SSH transport and
  `ss/netstat` output.
- `DeleteTunnelProfile` now stops all runtimes for that profile and refuses to
  delete the SQLite profile if port release cannot be verified.
- Added `RemoteForwardAccessRestartRequest` and
  `RemoteForwardAccessRestartResult`.
- Added `EnableRemoteForwardAccessAndRestart` Wails API.
- Manager flow for GatewayPorts restart:
  1. stop current remote listener;
  2. verify remote port release;
  3. run the existing GatewayPorts helper using the retained tunnel transport;
  4. close the old transport;
  5. restart the tunnel through normal `StartTunnel`;
  6. recheck actual remote listen state.
- GatewayPorts helper still modifies only `GatewayPorts yes`, still runs
  `sshd -t`, still backs up and rolls back on validation failure, and still
  rejects unsupported Dropbear/unknown cases.
- Tunnel UI now maps raw duplicate-name errors to the Chinese toast.
- Card actions moved into `.tunnel-card-header .tunnel-card-actions`.
- Cards use reduced padding and tighter runtime badges.
- Normal window layout pins the edit form to the left column and the profile
  list to the right column.
- Loopback-only action button now says `放行并重启`.
- Store removes the old tunnel runtime when the restart API returns the new
  runtime.
- Delete failure does not remove the profile from UI.

### Tests Added / Updated

- Persistence duplicate-name tests.
- Manager stop/release and GatewayPorts stop-before-enable test.
- Frontend duplicate-name error mapping test.
- Frontend card-header action placement test.
- Frontend delete failure keeps profile visible test.
- Frontend `放行并重启` flow calls the new restart API and does not call the old
  one-step `EnableRemoteForwardAccess`.

### Validation

- `gofmt`: passed.
- `go vet ./...`: passed.
- `go test ./...`: passed.
- `cmd.exe /c npm run type-check` from `frontend`: passed.
- `cmd.exe /c npm run test -- --run` from `frontend`: passed, 41 files and
  306 tests. Vitest still prints the existing jsdom canvas warning.
- `cmd.exe /c npm run build` from `frontend`: passed with existing Vite
  chunk-size warning.
- `wails build`: passed for Windows amd64.
- `git diff --check`: passed after cleaning generated Wails whitespace.

### User Manual Test Checklist

1. Create two same-name tunnel profiles under one server and confirm the Chinese
   duplicate-name toast.
2. Edit an existing profile without changing its name and confirm save succeeds.
3. Confirm profile cards are shorter and actions are top-right.
4. Confirm edit form is left and created profile list is right.
5. Create remote forward `0.0.0.0:12380 -> 192.168.0.252:22`.
6. When loopback-only, click `放行并重启`.
7. Confirm tunnel stops first and the remote port releases.
8. Confirm GatewayPorts yes is written, `sshd -t` passes, sshd reloads, and the
   tunnel restarts.
9. Confirm recheck public hides the old warning/help/button.
10. Delete running local/remote/SOCKS tunnel profiles and verify ports are no
    longer reachable/listening.
11. Confirm SSH/SFTP/SCP/Monitor/Local Terminal are unaffected.

### Still Not Done

- Firewall/LAN reachability automation.
- Dropbear/OpenWrt GatewayPorts editing.
- Interactive sudo password prompt.
- Remote release verification requires `ss` or `netstat`; without both tools,
  deletion is blocked because release cannot be proven.

## 2026-06-18: Tunnel UX Simplification and Lifecycle Hardening Follow-up

Current baseline before this stage:
`50cc3d9a6a272257ebdf50c2ec978891ebe608a1`.

reported from real use.

Windows executable: `build/bin/ServerPilot.exe`

Windows executable SHA-256:
`9340C538AC7E134476E7BB6A8D1AF28ED2F6A480E477990DEE6CE8688724147F`

### User Feedback Addressed

1. GatewayPorts enable followed by successful public recheck still left old
   loopback-only warning/help/button visible.
2. The `高级选项` foldout was unnecessary.

   buttons.

   longer reachable.

  button.

  update for the original profile.

- GatewayPorts public exposure now wins over stale loopback state in the UI:

## 2026-06-18: Tunnel Lifecycle, Edit Flow, GatewayPorts Helper, and Config UX

Current baseline before this stage:
`bb4526762324c95c8769397d9a185db4eb1acd85`.

Stage: harden tunnel profile deletion/editing lifecycle, add a guarded
GatewayPorts helper for loopback-only remote forwards, and simplify the tunnel
configuration UI.

Windows executable: `build/bin/ServerPilot.exe`

Windows executable SHA-256:
`8BFE4A23DC929AE040488BEE77B8EF28AC1860C824C2D6E8F4AD3ADC35F43B3A`

### User Feedback Addressed

1. Deleting a tunnel profile while it is running left the listener reachable.
2. Editing an existing profile and changing type could create a duplicate
   profile instead of updating the original.
3. Loopback-only remote forwards needed an explicit way to enable
   `GatewayPorts yes`, because the user's real test showed GatewayPorts alone
   fixed LAN reachability.
4. The tunnel configuration panel was too technical and needed scenario-based
   choices.

### Scope

- Changed tunnel manager/profile lifecycle, tunnel typed APIs, tunnel UI/store,
  Wails bindings, and tests.
- Did not modify SSH terminal runtime.
- Did not modify Terminal PTY lifecycle.
- Did not modify Monitor runtime.
- Did not modify SFTP/SCP runtime.
- Did not modify Local Terminal runtime.
- Did not modify `DisconnectServer(serverID)` semantics.
- Did not modify workspace state model.
- Did not modify SecretStore / Key Vault.
- Did not modify Backup / Import / Export secret behavior.

### Implemented

- Added `Manager.StopProfile(profileID)` and profile worker lookup.
- `App.DeleteTunnelProfile(id)` now stops all running tunnel workers for that
  profile before deleting the stored profile.
- The stop path closes listeners, active connections, transport, worker context,
  waits for accept-loop completion, and emits stopped state through the existing
  tunnel lifecycle.
- Frontend tunnel store now tombstones deleted profile IDs, removes matching
  runtimes immediately, and ignores late state events for deleted profiles.
- Edit mode preserves `profileID` through type changes.
- Type changes in edit mode no longer reset the form to create mode.
- Running profile edits show a confirm dialog. Confirmed saves stop the old
  runtime, update the original profile, and restart the updated tunnel.
- Remote forward scenario defaults to `0.0.0.0:12380` for server-open listener.
- Added domain/API types:
  - `RemoteForwardAccessRequest`;
  - `RemoteForwardAccessInspectResult`;
  - `RemoteForwardAccessEnableResult`.
- Added Wails APIs:
  - `InspectRemoteForwardAccess`;
  - `EnableRemoteForwardAccess`.
- Added OpenSSH GatewayPorts helper:
  - detects OpenSSH/Dropbear;
  - locates `/etc/ssh/sshd_config` or `/etc/sshd_config`;
  - checks effective `gatewayports` and `allowtcpforwarding` via `sshd -T`;
  - requires root or `sudo -n`;
  - creates `*.serverpilot.bak.<timestamp>` backup;
  - writes/replaces only `GatewayPorts yes`;
  - does not force-modify `AllowTcpForwarding`;
  - avoids writing inside `Match` blocks by inserting before the first Match
    when needed;
  - validates with `sshd -t`;
  - rolls back from backup on validation failure;
  - attempts `systemctl/service/init.d` reload commands;
  - returns warning if reload fails after config validation succeeds.
- Loopback-only remote cards now show `启用远程访问`. The button requires a
  danger confirmation and then rechecks the remote listener after the helper
  returns.
- Config form now uses scenario cards:
  - `访问服务器上的服务` for local forwarding;
  - `让服务器访问我电脑上的服务` for remote forwarding;
  - `SOCKS5 代理` for dynamic forwarding.
- Name and auto-start moved into `高级选项`.

### Security Notes

- The GatewayPorts helper does not log sshd_config contents.
- The helper does not log tunnel traffic, terminal input/output, passwords,
  private keys, passphrases, or tokens.
- No automatic silent server config mutation: UI confirmation is mandatory.
- Dropbear/OpenWrt config editing is intentionally unsupported in this round.
- No sudo password prompt is implemented; only root or passwordless sudo can
  run the helper.

### Tests Added / Updated

- `StopProfile` releases local, remote, and dynamic listeners while leaving
  unrelated profile runtimes alive.
- `StopTunnel` remains idempotent and closes active connections.
- GatewayPorts inspect parses OpenSSH and Dropbear marker outputs.
- GatewayPorts enable checks backup, `GatewayPorts yes`, `sshd -t`, rollback,
  success, validation failure, and unsupported Dropbear errors.
- Store deletes matching runtimes and ignores late deleted-profile state events.
- Store wraps inspect/enable GatewayPorts APIs.
- UI shows scenario cards and simplified labels.
- UI public remote bind still requires confirmation before start.
- UI edit type-change calls update, not create, and does not duplicate profile.
- UI running edit cancel does not save; confirm stops, updates, and restarts.
- UI delete running profile confirms and removes runtime state.
- UI loopback-only card shows GatewayPorts diagnostics, recheck, and
  `启用远程访问`.
- UI GatewayPorts helper cancel does not call backend; confirm calls backend and
  rechecks listener.

### Validation

- `go vet ./...`: passed.
- `go test ./...`: passed.
- `cmd.exe /c npm run type-check` from `frontend`: passed.
- `cmd.exe /c npm run test -- --run` from `frontend`: passed, 41 files and
  299 tests.
- `cmd.exe /c npm run build` from `frontend`: passed with the existing Vite
  chunk-size warning.
- `wails build`: passed for Windows amd64.
- `git diff --check`: passed.

2. If actual listen is loopback-only, confirm UI still shows
   `运行中（仅服务器本机）`.
3. Confirm the card shows `启用远程访问`.
4. Click it and verify the danger confirmation appears before any config change.
5. Confirm and verify sshd_config backup was created.
6. Confirm `GatewayPorts yes` was added/replaced.
7. Confirm `AllowTcpForwarding` was not force-modified.
8. Confirm `sshd -t` runs before reload.
9. If validation fails, confirm sshd_config is restored from backup.

## 2026-06-18: Remote Tunnel Loopback-Only Listener Classification

Current baseline before this stage:
`a12123aae317d865133426c569fa7e51ebc49eaa`.

Stage: classify remote tunnel listener exposure so loopback-only remote
forwards do not look like normal LAN-accessible running tunnels.

Windows executable: `build/bin/ServerPilot.exe`

Windows executable SHA-256:
`56CCEFF1E7CDA4DC1B926CBF128338B1E7B00EB217EF9D249D989AF59896EFE5`

### User Evidence

User created:
`0.0.0.0:12380 -> 192.168.0.252:22`

Server-side command:
`ss -ltn | grep 12380`

Result:

```text
LISTEN 0 128 127.0.0.1:12380 0.0.0.0:*
LISTEN 0 128 [::1]:12380 [::]:*
```

StopTunnel released the port correctly. The issue is not listener lifecycle; it
is that sshd only exposes the listener on loopback.

### Scope

- Changed tunnel domain state, tunnel listen parser, tunnel UI/store tests, and
  docs.
- Did not modify SSH terminal runtime.
- Did not modify SFTP/SCP runtime.
- Did not modify Monitor runtime.
- Did not modify Local Terminal runtime.
- Did not modify `DisconnectServer(serverID)` external semantics.
- Did not modify SecretStore / Key Vault.
- Did not modify Backup / Import / Export.

### Implemented

- Added `RemoteListenExposure`:
  - `public`;
  - `loopback_only`;
  - `not_listening`;
  - `unknown`.
- Added `effectiveListenAddrs []string` to `TunnelRuntime`.
- Parser now preserves all detected listen addresses for the requested port.
- Correctly classifies:
  - `127.0.0.1:12380` as loopback-only;
  - `[::1]:12380` as loopback-only;
  - `0.0.0.0:12380` as public;
  - `[::]:12380` as public;
  - `192.168.0.88:12380` as public on a specific host.
- If loopback and public entries coexist, exposure is public.
- Requested public bind with loopback-only actual bind gets GatewayPorts
  warning.
- UI status labels now show:
  - `运行中（仅服务器本机）`;
  - `运行中（局域网可访问）`;
  - `运行中（未确认监听）`.
- Loopback-only is warning styled, not normal green running.
- Loopback-only help explains:
  - `AllowTcpForwarding yes`;
  - `GatewayPorts yes`;
  - or `GatewayPorts clientspecified`;
  - restart sshd;
  - check firewall for the remote port.
- Test command generation now uses actual exposure:
  - loopback SSH: `ssh -p PORT root@127.0.0.1`;
  - public SSH: `ssh -p PORT root@serverIP`;
  - loopback HTTP: `curl http://127.0.0.1:PORT`;
  - public HTTP: `curl http://serverIP:PORT`.
- `重新检测` still updates actual listen, exposure, warning, and command.

### Tests Added / Updated

- Parse `127.0.0.1:PORT` as loopback-only.
- Parse `[::1]:PORT` as loopback-only.
- Parse `0.0.0.0:PORT` as public.
- Parse `[::]:PORT` as public.
- Parse specific non-loopback host bind as public.
- Public listener wins over loopback when both exist.
- Requested public bind plus loopback-only actual bind gets GatewayPorts
  warning.
- Loopback-only startup remains running, not failed.
- `not_listening` startup still fails.
- Target port 22 loopback command uses `127.0.0.1`.
- Target port 22 public command uses server host.
- HTTP loopback/public commands use `curl http://host:port`.
- UI shows `运行中（仅服务器本机）`, GatewayPorts help, multiple actual
  listen addresses, and refreshed public status after recheck.

### Validation

- `gofmt`: passed.
- `go vet ./...`: passed.
- `go test ./...`: passed.
- `cmd.exe /c npm run type-check` from `frontend`: passed.
- `cmd.exe /c npm run test -- --run` from `frontend`: passed, 41 files and
  291 tests.
- `cmd.exe /c npm run build` from `frontend`: passed with the existing Vite
  chunk-size warning.
- `wails build`: passed for Windows amd64.
- `git diff --check`: passed.

### User Manual Test Checklist

1. Create remote forward `0.0.0.0:12380 -> 192.168.0.252:22`.
2. Start the tunnel.
3. On server, run `ss -ltn | grep 12380`.
4. If actual listeners are `127.0.0.1:12380` and `[::1]:12380`, UI must show
   `运行中（仅服务器本机）`.
5. UI must show GatewayPorts help.
6. UI test command must be `ssh -p 12380 root@127.0.0.1`.
7. Windows external refused access to `192.168.0.88:12380` must not be shown
   as LAN-accessible.
8. Stop the tunnel and confirm the port is released.

### Still Not Done

- ServerPilot only explains sshd/GatewayPorts changes; it does not edit server
  configuration.
- Firewall and LAN reachability still require real testing after server config
  changes.

## 2026-06-18: Remote Tunnel Listener Verification

Current baseline before this stage:
`b824133d698a877bdfd74cc68dd429afc0f86141`.

Stage: fix remote SSH port forwarding showing `运行中` while the actual remote
port refused external connections.

Windows executable: `build/bin/ServerPilot.exe`

Windows executable SHA-256:
`AFBC6887B06C419D29183BF2FDBCF961447F3DD07D436E154BBB16CF23EFA56C`

### Scope

- Changed only tunnel manager/domain/API/UI/test surfaces and handoff docs.
- Did not modify SSH terminal runtime.
- Did not modify SFTP/SCP runtime.
- Did not modify Monitor runtime.
- Did not modify Local Terminal runtime.
- Did not change `DisconnectServer(serverID)` external semantics.
- Did not modify workspace state model.
- Did not modify SecretStore or Key Vault.
- Did not modify Backup / Import / Export.
- Tunnel runtime remains independent and uses its own SSH client.

### Root Cause

- Remote tunnel startup marked the runtime as running immediately after
  `ssh.Client.Listen("tcp", requestedBind)` succeeded.
- The UI had no way to distinguish:
  - a real wildcard/public listener;
  - a loopback-only listener caused by sshd `GatewayPorts`;
  - no visible remote listener;
  - unknown state because `ss/netstat` is unavailable.
- That allowed a false green state: `0.0.0.0:12380 -> ...` looked running even
  when `ssh -p 12380 root@192.168.0.88` returned `connection refused`.

### Implemented

- Added `RemoteListenCheckStatus`:
  `unchecked`, `listening`, `loopback_only`, `not_listening`, `unknown`.
- Added `TunnelRuntime` fields:
  requested listen, actual listen, effective remote bind host, listen check
  status, warning, and test command.
- Extended the tunnel transport with a diagnostic `Run(ctx, command)` method
  using the existing SSH client `Run` helper.
- After creating a remote listener, tunnel manager now executes:
  - `ss -ltn`;
  - fallback `netstat -ltn`;
  - missing command marker.
- Parser recognizes `127.0.0.1:PORT`, `0.0.0.0:PORT`, `[::]:PORT`,
  `:::PORT`, `*:PORT`, and specific IP binds.
- If no requested port is detected, startup fails, listener/transport are
  closed, and the runtime cannot remain ordinary running.
- If requested bind is public but actual bind is loopback, runtime stays running
  with a GatewayPorts warning.
- If detection tools are unavailable, runtime stays running with `unknown`
  warning.
- Added typed Wails API `CheckTunnelRemoteListen`.
- Added `重新检测` in the tunnel UI.
- Remote tunnel cards now display requested listen, actual listen, status,
  warning, and protocol-aware test command:
  - target port 22: `ssh -p PORT user@HOST`;
  - common HTTP ports: `curl http://HOST:PORT/`;
  - other ports: `nc -vz HOST PORT`.

### Tests Added / Updated

- Remote listener stays open after `StartTunnel` returns.
- `StopTunnel` closes the remote listener.
- `not_listening` fails startup and does not leave a running tunnel.
- missing `ss/netstat` produces `unknown` warning without crash.
- public requested bind plus loopback actual bind produces GatewayPorts warning.
- parser handles wildcard, loopback, IPv6 wildcard, missing port, and missing
  tool output.
- test command generation covers SSH and HTTP targets.
- store API calls `CheckTunnelRemoteListen`.
- dialog shows requested/actual listen, GatewayPorts warning, test command, and
  updates after `重新检测`.

### Validation

- `gofmt`: passed.
- `go vet ./...`: passed.
- `go test ./...`: passed.
- `cmd.exe /c npm run type-check` from `frontend`: passed.
- `cmd.exe /c npm run test -- --run` from `frontend`: passed, 41 files and
  291 tests.
- `cmd.exe /c npm run build` from `frontend`: passed with the existing Vite
  chunk-size warning.
- `wails build`: passed for Windows amd64.
- `git diff --check`: passed.

### User Manual Test Checklist

1. Create remote forward `0.0.0.0:12380 -> 192.168.0.252:22`.
2. Start the tunnel.
3. Confirm the card shows requested listen `0.0.0.0:12380`.
4. Confirm actual listen is shown as wildcard/public, loopback-only, not found,
   or unknown.
5. On server `192.168.0.88`, run `ss -ltn | grep 12380`.
6. If actual listen is loopback, run in server SSH terminal:
   `ssh -p 12380 root@127.0.0.1`.
7. If actual listen is wildcard/public, run from Windows:
   `ssh -p 12380 root@192.168.0.88`.
8. Click `重新检测` and confirm actual listen / warning / command updates.
9. Stop the tunnel and confirm remote port release.
10. Confirm SSH terminal, SFTP/SCP, Monitor, Local Terminal, SecretStore, Key
    Vault, Backup / Export remain unaffected.

### Still Not Done

- Remote listener detection does not prove firewall access from every outside
  client network.
- If both `ss` and `netstat` are unavailable, status is intentionally
  `unknown` with warning instead of false failure.
- No tunnel auto-start profile behavior was added.

## 2026-06-18: Direct Windows ConPTY Adapter Implemented But Still Gated

Current baseline before this stage:
`25a0072d6e4dce4117014d4f2c00136ecb74a891`.

Stage: implement a true Windows Direct ConPTY adapter and enable Local
Terminal only if PowerShell and cmd pass real enabled GUI smoke. Result:
Direct ConPTY was implemented, but Local Terminal remains
default-disabled/quarantined because all release conditions were not met.

Windows executable: `build/bin/ServerPilot.exe`

Windows executable SHA-256:
`403939C30DF232DF564139160E4CA08B0CEF937F11624FB5FAE849CC65DAEE8C`

### Scope

- Did not modify SSH runtime architecture.
- Did not modify SSH Terminal PTY lifecycle.
- Did not modify Monitor runtime.
- Did not modify SFTP/SCP runtime.
- Did not modify `DisconnectServer(serverID)`.
- Did not modify workspace state model.
- Did not modify SecretStore or Key Vault.
- Did not modify Backup / Import / Export.
- Did not modify Command History / Favorites / Completion data model.
- Local Terminal remains independent from `serverID` and is not part of
  `DisconnectServer(serverID)`.

### Root Cause

- The previous Windows Local Terminal path was not a project-owned Direct
  ConPTY adapter. It relied on the manager-level third-party PTY wrapper path.
- The new Direct ConPTY path works in a backend cmd smoke test.
- A reproducible comparison showed `CREATE_NO_WINDOW` breaks ConPTY I/O on this
  Windows runtime:
  - no extra flags: cmd echo works;
  - `CREATE_NEW_PROCESS_GROUP`: cmd echo works;
  - `CREATE_NO_WINDOW`: cmd echo times out;
  - both flags: cmd echo times out.
- Because the task required `CREATE_NO_WINDOW`, and enabled PowerShell/cmd GUI
  smoke was not executed and passed, exposing Local Terminal would be unsafe.

### Implemented

- Added a Windows-only Direct ConPTY adapter under `internal/localterminal`.
- The adapter uses:
  - `CreatePseudoConsole`;
  - `ResizePseudoConsole`;
  - `ClosePseudoConsole`;
  - `STARTUPINFOEX`;
  - `PROC_THREAD_ATTRIBUTE_PSEUDOCONSOLE`;
  - direct `InitializeProcThreadAttributeList`;
  - direct `UpdateProcThreadAttribute`;
  - direct `DeleteProcThreadAttributeList`;
  - `CreateProcessW`;
  - `EXTENDED_STARTUPINFO_PRESENT`;
  - `CREATE_NEW_PROCESS_GROUP`;
  - UTF-16 command line;
  - owned stdin/stdout pipes.
- Removed the Windows dependency on the previous go-pty manager path.
- Kept `stableLocalTerminalReleaseGate = false`.
- `OpenLocalTerminal` still returns `LOCAL_TERMINAL_DISABLED`.
- `+` menu and Settings Local Terminal controls remain hidden.
- Added sanitized lifecycle logs for local terminal open/exit/close:
  sessionID, shell basename, PID, state, and exit code only.
- No terminal input, output, cwd, command text, environment, password, token, or
  private-key material is logged.
- Non-Windows PTY startup is kept behind the same `startPlatformPTY` boundary
  without touching SSH terminal runtime.

### Tests Added / Updated

- `TestWindowsDirectConPTYAdapterUsesRequiredAPIs` verifies the adapter source
  uses the Direct ConPTY API surface.
- `TestEmbeddedPTYCreationFlagsUseIndependentProcessGroup` verifies the local
  terminal process uses an independent process group and documents that
  `CREATE_NO_WINDOW` is not enabled because it breaks I/O here.
- `TestWindowsAdapterDoesNotBroadcastConsoleControlEvent` continues to ensure
  no `GenerateConsoleCtrlEvent`, `CTRL_C_EVENT`, or `CTRL_BREAK_EVENT` path is
  present.
- `TestDirectConPTYCmdEchoSmoke` creates Direct ConPTY, starts `cmd.exe`,
  resizes, writes `echo serverpilot-conpty-unit-smoke`, reads the marker,
  sends `exit`, and waits for process exit.
- Base64 output contract coverage remains in place.

### Windows GUI Smoke

Default-disabled smoke was executed:

1. Built `build/bin/ServerPilot.exe`.
2. Started the EXE.
3. Waited until a real main window handle existed.
4. Closed the window via `WM_CLOSE`, not process kill.
5. Confirmed normal process exit.
6. Confirmed child processes after close: 0.
7. Confirmed shell child processes after close: 0.
8. Confirmed residual new `ServerPilot.exe` processes after close: 0.

Enabled Local Terminal smoke was not executed:

- PowerShell enabled GUI smoke: not executed.
- cmd enabled GUI smoke: not executed.
- External-window behavior in enabled mode: not assessed.
- shell `exit` behavior in enabled GUI mode: not assessed.
- local tab close behavior in enabled GUI mode: not assessed.

Reason: the release conditions were not met because the required
`CREATE_NO_WINDOW` flag conflicts with ConPTY I/O in the reproducible backend
comparison.

### Validation

- `gofmt`: passed.
- `go test ./internal/localterminal -count=1 -v -timeout=60s`: passed,
  including `TestDirectConPTYCmdEchoSmoke`.
- `go vet ./...`: passed.
- `go test ./...`: passed.
- `cmd.exe /c npm run type-check` from `frontend`: passed.
- `cmd.exe /c npm run test -- --run` from `frontend`: passed, 39 files and
  274 tests.
- `cmd.exe /c npm run build` from `frontend`: passed with the existing Vite
  chunk-size warning.
- `wails build`: passed for Windows amd64.
- `git diff --check`: passed.

### User Manual Test Checklist

1. Open the `+` menu and confirm Local Terminal is still absent.
2. Open Settings and confirm Local Terminal shell preference is still hidden.
3. Close ServerPilot normally and confirm no residual ServerPilot process
   remains.
4. Confirm no `cmd.exe`, `powershell.exe`, or `pwsh.exe` child process is left
   by ServerPilot.
5. Confirm SSH terminal, SFTP/SCP, Monitor, Backup, and Command panels are
   unchanged.

### Still Not Done

- Local Terminal is not available to users.
- Enabled PowerShell GUI smoke has not passed.
- Enabled cmd GUI smoke has not passed.
- A product decision is needed for the `CREATE_NO_WINDOW` conflict: keep that
  explicit flag requirement and stay disabled, or separately approve the
  working Direct ConPTY launch shape that omits the flag.

## 2026-06-18: Append New Terminal Tabs To The Right

Current baseline before this stage:
`fd6a7aa71b21ab8af98d23bcf27148138b0eabd1`.

Stage: fix UI topbar tab ordering so newly opened server/terminal tabs append
to the far right. This is a UI ordering fix only.

Windows executable: `build/bin/ServerPilot.exe`

Windows executable SHA-256:
`95D9AECED66EAD5576E902EF46A747D68201EF405A0235F9DAB6A07E49F4755E`

### Scope

- Changed only `WorkspaceTabs.vue`, `WorkspaceTabs.test.ts`, and docs.

### Implemented

- Topbar terminal tabs now render in `store.tabs` global order.
- This preserves append semantics because new terminal sessions are pushed to
  `store.tabs`.
- A same-server new terminal now appears at the far right:
  `A | B | A #2`, not `A | A #2 | B`.
- A newly opened server terminal also appears at the far right:

  `#2` remain accurate.

  sessions.

  - final server tab close still triggers server-wide disconnect.

### Root Cause

- `WorkspaceTabs.vue` previously iterated `workspaceOrder` first and rendered
  all terminal sessions under each server.
- That grouped same-server terminals together even when the terminal session was
  created later.
- Global append order was already present in `store.tabs`; the UI simply was
  not using it as the primary display order.

  - added coverage for appending a same-server terminal to the right;
  - added coverage for appending a newly opened server terminal to the far
    right;

## 2026-06-18: Final Terminal Tab Server Disconnect Lifecycle Fix

Current baseline before this stage:
`897ddb468b50d38b3310a6ccc6e6eea41e267d3c`.

Stage: fix the lifecycle bug where closing every visible server/terminal tab
could leave the server runtime online, the `+` menu green, and monitor data
still visible. This stage attaches final-tab close to the existing
`DisconnectServer(serverID)` lifecycle without changing runtime architecture.

Windows executable: `build/bin/ServerPilot.exe`

Windows executable SHA-256:
`17926C6DAF0CE7B44366D89DF3B074BCA089CCF58DA4BD82334E1825CD67CB71`

### Scope

- Changed final terminal tab close decision logic, App final-disconnect state
  cleanup, frontend SFTP late-event suppression, tests, and docs.
- Did not modify SSH runtime architecture.
- Did not modify Terminal backend PTY lifecycle.
- Did not modify Monitor runtime architecture.
- Did not modify SFTP/SCP runtime architecture.
- Did not modify backend `DisconnectServer(serverID)` semantics.
- Did not modify workspace state model broadly.
- Did not modify SecretStore, Key Vault, or Backup / Import / Export behavior.
- Local Terminal remains default-disabled/quarantined.

### Implemented

- Final terminal tab close
  - `WorkspaceTabs.vue` now checks
    `sessionsByServerId[serverID].length <= 1` before closing a terminal tab.
  - If the same server has other terminal tabs, the old session-scoped behavior
    remains:
    `CloseTerminal(sessionID)` plus the bound `CloseSftpContext`.
  - If this is the server's final terminal tab, the UI removes the server
    workspace immediately and emits `finalTerminalDisconnect(serverID)`.
  - `TerminalWorkspace.vue` uses the same final-tab decision for close events
    emitted by terminal views/context-menu paths.
- App lifecycle hookup
  - `App.vue` handles `finalTerminalDisconnect` by using the existing
    `disconnectServer(connection, closeWorkspace=true)` path with immediate
    frontend cleanup.
  - The immediate cleanup clears SFTP/SCP state, marks connection state
    disconnected, clears monitor snapshot/history, and suppresses stale
    connection/monitor events before the backend API returns.
  - Backend `DisconnectServer(serverID)` remains the single server-wide runtime
    shutdown entry.
- SFTP frontend cleanup
  - `sftpStore.clearServer(serverID)` now deletes stale
    `stateByServerId[serverID]`.
  - The SFTP store tracks suppressed server IDs after `clearServer`.
  - Late `sftp:state`, `sftp:entries`, `sftp:error`, and `sftp:transfer` events
    for a suppressed server are ignored.
  - `open`, `markAuthRequired`, and `ensureContext` resume SFTP event
    acceptance for explicit reconnect/open flows.

### Root Cause

- The prior fix solved the visual two-click tab shell issue but kept final-tab
  close on the terminal-only path.
- That path called `CloseTerminal(sessionID)` and the terminal-bound
  `CloseSftpContext`, but it did not call the server-wide
  `DisconnectServer(serverID)` lifecycle.
- As a result, Monitor and other server-scoped runtime state could remain
  active, and the server store could keep stale online connection state.
- The `+` menu uses connection runtime state, so stale online state kept the
  server green.
- SFTP frontend state also retained stale server-level `online` state because
  `clearServer` did not remove `stateByServerId`.

### Tests Added / Updated

- `frontend/src/components/WorkspaceTabs.test.ts`
  - non-final terminal close stays session-scoped;
  - final terminal close emits final server disconnect and does not call
    `CloseTerminal`;

## 2026-06-18: Per-Server Final Terminal Tab Close Fix

Current baseline before this stage:
`22288dcc2caf01cac165af79c878613019433098`.

Stage: fix the remaining terminal-tab close regression where each server's
last terminal tab needed two close attempts. This stage only changes frontend
terminal/workspace tab cleanup semantics and tests.

Windows executable: `build/bin/ServerPilot.exe`

Windows executable SHA-256:
`408448FE65F13C5843AE38778390882AEE3DEBEE9FC7D1D0CF2AFEEC94E64132`

### Scope

- Changed `frontend/src/stores/terminal.ts` and focused frontend tests.
- Did not modify SSH runtime architecture.
- Did not modify Terminal backend PTY lifecycle.
- Did not modify Monitor runtime.
- Did not modify SFTP runtime architecture.
- Did not modify `DisconnectServer(serverID)` semantics.
- Did not modify workspace state model broadly.
- Did not modify SecretStore, Key Vault, or Backup / Import / Export behavior.
- Local Terminal remains default-disabled/quarantined.

### Implemented

- Added a `closeSession(sessionID)`-scoped workspace-shell cleanup path.
- When the closed terminal is the last terminal for its `serverID`, the store
  now removes that server from:
  - `workspaces`;
  - `workspaceOrder`;
  - `workspaceHistory`;
  - `lastActiveTerminalByServer`.
- The close path no longer calls `updateWorkspace(serverID, 'disconnected')`
  for a final terminal-tab close.
- If the closed tab has sibling terminal tabs on other servers, active fallback
  stays on the adjacent/remaining terminal tab selected by the existing
  terminal fallback logic.
- If no terminal tab remains, active terminal/workspace state is cleared and no
  fake offline top tab is created.
- Backend close failure still restores the prior frontend tab/workspace state
  and persists the restored workspace order.
- Explicit `disconnectServer(connectionId, false)` still preserves the
  disconnected workspace because generic `removeSessions()` was not changed.
- Terminal close remains session-scoped:
  - `CloseTerminal(sessionID)`;
  - matching `CloseSftpContext(serverID, contextID=sessionID)`;
  - no `DisconnectServer(serverID)`.
- Existing closed-session tombstones still suppress late terminal output/status
  events.

### Root Cause

- The previous fix handled the global final-tab case by clearing active state,
  but it left the server's workspace record in `workspaces/workspaceOrder`.
- `WorkspaceTabs.vue` renders a workspace-only top tab whenever a workspace has
  no terminal sessions.
- Therefore the first close removed the terminal session but immediately
  exposed the remaining disconnected workspace shell. The second click removed
  that shell.
- This was per-server: each server could reproduce the two-click behavior when
  its own final terminal tab was closed.

### Tests Added / Updated

- `frontend/src/stores/terminal.test.ts`
  - final terminal close removes the workspace shell and workspace order entry;
  - late terminal status does not revive the removed tab/workspace;
  - closing server B's final terminal while server A still has terminals removes
    only server B's workspace shell;
  - closing an inactive server's final terminal does not switch away from the
    active terminal;
  - close-current-terminal still does not call `DisconnectServer`.
- `frontend/src/components/WorkspaceTabs.test.ts`
  - final tab close button removes the visible top tab with one click;
  - another server's final terminal close does not leave a disconnected
    workspace tab;
  - context-menu "close current terminal" on a final tab uses the same
    one-action path;
  - explicit server disconnect remains the only menu action that emits
    `disconnectServer`.

- `go vet ./...`: passed.
- `go test ./...`: passed.
- `cmd.exe /c npm run type-check` from `frontend`: passed.
- `cmd.exe /c npm run test -- --run` from `frontend`: passed, 39 files and
  269 tests.
- `cmd.exe /c npm run build` from `frontend`: passed with the existing Vite
  chunk-size warning.
- `wails build`: passed for Windows amd64.
- `git diff --check`: passed after cleaning Wails-generated trailing whitespace.

2. Click the tab close button once.
3. Confirm the tab disappears immediately.
4. Confirm no disconnected/offline server A top tab remains.
5. Confirm no second click is required.
6. Open server A with three terminal tabs.
7. Close each tab once and confirm each disappears on the first click.
8. Confirm the final server A tab also disappears on the first click.
9. Open server B with one terminal tab while server A still has a terminal.
10. Close server B's final terminal tab once.
11. Confirm server B's tab disappears and server A remains active/usable.
12. Right-click a final terminal tab and select "close current terminal".
13. Confirm it closes once and does not call server-wide disconnect.
14. Confirm SFTP context cleanup is limited to the closed terminal context.
15. Confirm SSH terminal, Monitor, Backup, Settings overlay, and Local Terminal
    quarantine are unchanged.

## 2026-06-18: 5-Item Interaction Fixes, SCP Compatibility, And Settings Overlay

Current baseline before this stage:
`9e64319fdff2a08401ed04ce50d1145b9bb90f81`.

Stage: terminal tab close reliability, shell-based SCP compatibility file
management, command panel double-click insert/history edit, and Settings
floating overlay with categories. This stage attaches capabilities to existing
managers/UI only.

Windows executable: `build/bin/ServerPilot.exe`

Windows executable SHA-256:
`C9AAC4EA42694C3D8C3BC6178162600B56F7CEE70C5C44A1671B741795ED5012`

### Scope

- Changed terminal tab close UI/store behavior, SFTP context cleanup, SCP
  compatibility manager routes, command history backend/API/UI, Settings UI
  shell, generated Wails bindings, tests, and docs.
- Did not modify SSH runtime architecture.
- Did not modify Terminal backend PTY lifecycle.
- Did not modify Monitor runtime.
- Did not modify `DisconnectServer(serverID)` semantics.
- Did not modify workspace state model.
- Did not modify SecretStore, Key Vault, or Backup / Import / Export secret
  behavior.
- Local Terminal remains default-disabled/quarantined.

### Implemented

- Terminal tab close
  - `terminal.closeSession(sessionID)` now removes the tab optimistically and
    restores previous state if backend close fails.
  - `WorkspaceTabs` closes the matching SFTP context for that terminal session
    with `CloseSftpContext`.
  - One-terminal close does not call `DisconnectServer(serverID)`.
  - Context-menu "close current terminal" uses the same one-click close path.
- SCP compatibility mode
  - Added `internal/sftpmanager/scp_shell_compat.go`.
  - SCP mode capabilities now advertise shell-backed browse/manage/edit when
    shell compatibility works.
  - Backend routes SCP-mode list, stat, mkdir, same-directory rename, inspect
    delete, delete, read text, and write text through independent SSH exec
    commands owned by the SFTP manager.
  - Frontend SFTP panel no longer disables file list/edit/properties/rename/
    delete/mkdir/refresh simply because `mode=scp`; it only enters limited mode
    when shell browse fails.
  - Shell compatibility failures degrade to limited SCP mode in the frontend
    without affecting terminal or monitor workers.
- Command panel
  - History double-click inserts into the current active SSH terminal and does
    not send Enter.
  - Favorite double-click inserts and updates use count; explicit Execute still
    sends Enter.
  - Added typed `UpdateCommandHistory(request)` Wails API and persistence path.
  - History row hover action and context menu can open a small edit modal.
  - Backend reuses existing command safety checks for edited history.
- Settings overlay
  - Top menu Settings opens `SettingsView` as an overlay over the current page.
  - `activeView` stays on the previous page, so the terminal workspace remains
    mounted and xterm state is preserved.
  - Settings categories group existing cards without changing the settings data
    model.
  - Save action remains sticky; close can use the existing app dialog flow for
    dirty forms.

### Design / Safety Notes

- SCP compatibility is not a terminal feature. It stays inside SFTP manager
  compatibility mode and uses the SFTP-owned independent SSH transport.
- Shell paths are passed through `QuotePOSIXArg` and remote target validation.
- Delete rejects empty path, root, `.`, `..`, parent traversal, and control
  characters.
- Text editing remains bounded to small UTF-8 text and does not log file
  contents.
- Command history edit does not change any remote command that already ran; it
  only updates local SQLite command-history metadata.
- Settings overlay is below modal/toast z-index and does not replace the main
  workspace.

### Tests Added / Updated

- Go:
  - `QuotePOSIXArg` escaping for spaces, Unicode, single quote, shell
    metacharacters, `$()`, and backticks.
  - SCP path validation and root-delete rejection.
  - SCP shell listing parser for file, directory, symlink, and unsafe names.
  - SCP manager route tests for list, mkdir, rename, inspect/delete, text
    read, and text write.
  - Command history update validation/service/persistence.
- Frontend:
  - Settings overlay/category rendering and close action.
  - App Settings overlay preserves mounted terminal workspace and unsubmitted
    input.
  - Command panel double-click insert for history and favorites.
  - Command history edit modal calls the typed backend update.
  - Workspace terminal close calls `CloseTerminal` and matching
    `CloseSftpContext` only.

- `go test ./...`: passed.

  262 tests.
- `cmd.exe /c npm run build` from `frontend`: passed with the existing Vite
  chunk-size warning.
- `wails build`: passed for Windows amd64.

2. Close the second terminal tab once; it should disappear immediately.
3. Confirm tabs one and three remain usable.
4. Confirm Monitor remains active and no server-wide disconnect occurs.
5. Confirm only the second terminal's SFTP context closes.

## 2026-06-18: SFTP / SCP Transfer Pause-Resume MVP

Current baseline before this stage:
`c11a5ce6311a394f05537700ad6446ffe8d709fa`.

Stage: implement transfer pause/resume for the existing bottom SFTP/SCP transfer
queue. This is an SFTP/SCP manager and UI extension only.

Windows executable: `build/bin/ServerPilot.exe`

Windows executable SHA-256:
`D1E9F107120FF3ECCAF58C57A26150CCD50C6189B8D9DC195F4C65A1F186A61E`

### Scope

- Changed SFTP domain transfer state, SFTP manager transfer control, recursive
  transfer copy loops, SCP fallback directory file-boundary pause hooks, Wails
  SFTP transfer control APIs, generated Wails bindings, SFTP frontend store,
  TerminalWorkspace transfer popover/status bar UI, tests, and docs.
- Did not modify SSH runtime architecture.
- Did not modify Terminal backend PTY lifecycle.
- Did not modify Monitor runtime.
- Did not modify `DisconnectServer(serverID)` semantics.
- Did not modify workspace state model.
- Did not modify SecretStore, Key Vault, or Backup / Import / Export behavior.
- Local Terminal remains default-disabled/quarantined.

### Implemented

- Backend transfer state
  - Added statuses: `pausing`, `paused`, `resuming`.
  - Added current file byte progress, `resumeOffset`, pause/cancel requested
    flags, and explicit `canPause/canResume/canCancel` flags.
  - Existing terminal/SFTP context isolation remains keyed by
    `serverID + contextID`.
- Wails/API/domain
  - Added typed `SFTPTransferControlRequest` and
    `SFTPTransferControlResponse`.
  - Added `SftpPauseTransfer(request)` and `SftpResumeTransfer(request)`.
  - Both APIs validate `serverID + contextID + transferID`.
  - Missing transfer IDs and mismatched contexts return Chinese errors.
- SFTP file transfer behavior
  - Upload/download copy loops check pause at chunk boundaries.
  - Paused state records offset and remains cancelable.
  - Resume seeks both source and destination to the stored offset.
  - Resume rejects obvious file changes rather than silently overwriting from
    the beginning.
- SFTP recursive transfer behavior
  - Recursive upload/download keeps the current in-memory plan.
  - Completed files are not restarted after resume.
  - Current file offset is preserved while paused.
- SCP fallback behavior
  - SCP directory transfers support cooperative pause at file boundaries.
  - Resume continues remaining files.
  - SCP does not claim arbitrary half-file offset resume.
  - UI shows the limitation with the Chinese message that SCP pauses after the
    current file completes.
- Frontend
  - SFTP store exposes `pauseTransfer` and `resumeTransfer`.
  - Store uses the transfer's own `connectionId/contextId`, not the active UI
    context, to prevent same-server terminal tab contamination.
  - Transfer popover shows pause/resume beside cancel.
  - Status bar shows paused state and a compact resume action.

### Root Cause / Design Notes

- Existing transfers could only be canceled. There was no control state between
  `running` and terminal states such as `completed`/`failed`/`canceled`.
- SFTP can safely resume from an offset because the manager controls both file
  handles and can seek both sides.
- SCP legacy mode cannot safely resume arbitrary half-file transfers, so the
  implementation deliberately limits SCP pause to cooperative file-boundary
  behavior.
- Pause/resume is deliberately runtime-only and is not written to Backup /
  Export.

### Tests Added / Updated

- Go:
  - SFTP file upload pause records offset and resumes to completion.
  - SFTP file download pause records offset and resumes to completion.
  - Pause/resume rejects mismatched context IDs.
  - Resume rejects missing transfer IDs.
  - Pausing one transfer does not mutate another transfer.
  - Paused transfer can be canceled without closing the SFTP session.
- Frontend:
  - SFTP store pause/resume uses the transfer's own context.
  - Transfer popover shows pause for running transfer.
  - Clicking pause calls `SftpPauseTransfer` with
    `serverID/contextID/transferID`.
  - Paused transfers still allow cancel.
  - Status bar shows paused state and calls `SftpResumeTransfer`.

### Validation

- `gofmt`: passed.
- `go vet ./...`: passed.
- `go test ./...`: passed.
- `cmd.exe /c npm run type-check`: passed.
- `cmd.exe /c npm run test -- --run`: passed, 39 files and 258 tests.
- `cmd.exe /c npm run build`: passed with existing Vite chunk-size warning.
- `wails build`: passed for Windows amd64.

### Manual Verification Checklist

1. Upload a large file, click pause, and confirm state becomes paused.
2. Resume the upload and confirm it continues from the paused offset.
3. Download a large file, click pause, resume, and confirm final file size is
   correct.
4. Upload a multi-level directory, pause halfway, and confirm resume does not
   upload completed files again.
5. Download a multi-level directory, pause halfway, and confirm resume does not
   download completed files again.
6. In SCP fallback directory transfer, click pause and confirm it pauses after
   the current file completes.
7. Confirm SCP does not advertise full arbitrary offset resume.
8. Confirm paused transfers can be canceled.
9. Confirm same-server terminal tab transfers do not affect each other.
10. Confirm `DisconnectServer(serverID)` still cancels all transfers for that
    server.
11. Confirm SSH terminal remains usable and focused.
12. Confirm SFTP context remains connected during pause/resume.
13. Confirm Local Terminal remains hidden by default.

## 2026-06-18: Same-Server Terminal SFTP Context Isolation And Command Scope Filter

Current baseline before this correction:
`8274b7df8fcd26b4a9928dc5d421cce03eda0801`.

Stage: continue correcting same-server multi-terminal workspace semantics and
Command panel scope filtering. This is not a new feature stage.

Windows executable: `build/bin/ServerPilot.exe`

Windows executable SHA-256:
`8956BA8E46112BBCFD93F16B77615B6B2ABC74B4EA0918E467B419440E200B56`

### Scope

- Changed SFTP typed domain models, SFTP manager session keying, SFTP Wails API
  requests/responses, generated Wails bindings, SFTP frontend store, SFTP panel
  API calls, TerminalWorkspace context binding, App SFTP auto-open/auth flow,
  Command history/favorite query scope, Command panel UI/store, tests, and docs.
- Did not modify SSH runtime architecture.
- Did not modify Terminal backend PTY lifecycle.
- Did not modify Monitor runtime.
- Did not modify `DisconnectServer(serverID)` semantics.
- Did not modify workspace state model.
- Did not modify SecretStore, Key Vault, or Backup / Import / Export behavior.
- Local Terminal remains default-disabled/quarantined.

### Implemented

- Backend SFTP manager
  - SFTP sessions and states are keyed by `serverID + contextID`.
  - `contextID` defaults to legacy `server:<id>` only when not provided.
  - `Open` accepts context and terminal session identifiers.
  - Same server can now own multiple independent SFTP sessions.
  - `StopContext(serverID, contextID)` closes one SFTP context only.
  - `Stop(serverID)` still closes all SFTP contexts for server-wide disconnect.
  - SFTP list/home/parent/stat/read/write/upload/download/recursive transfer,
    delete inspection, delete, errors, transfers, and SCP fallback preserve
    context.
- Wails/API/domain
  - Added `contextID` and `terminalSessionID` to SFTP request/state/result
    structs.
  - Added `CloseSftpContext` and `GetSftpContextState`.
  - Regenerated frontend Wails bindings and models.
- Frontend SFTP state
  - Added context-indexed state, entries, errors, selected paths, sorting,
    hidden-file toggle, upload refresh errors, and transfers.
  - Added `terminalSessionID -> sftpContextID` mapping and active context.
  - Late SFTP events update only their own context.
  - Upload-complete refresh selects/refreshes only the matching context.
- Terminal workspace
  - Active terminal tab drives `activeSftpContextID`.
  - Switching same-server terminal tabs switches the bottom SFTP panel context.
  - Closing one terminal tab closes only its SFTP context and terminal session.
  - Current transfer cleanup now applies to the active SFTP context.
- App flow
  - Auto-open SFTP after terminal creation uses that terminal's `sessionID` as
    the SFTP context.
  - Manual SFTP open uses the active or first terminal session context.
  - Auth dialog SFTP retry preserves the pending SFTP context.
- Command panel
  - Added `全部命令 / 当前服务器` segmented scope filter.
  - Default scope is `全部命令`, stored in non-sensitive local state.
  - All mode queries cross-server history/favorites and shows source badges.
  - Current-server mode queries only active server history plus applicable
    global/group/server favorites.
  - Insert/execute still targets only the active terminal and never switches or
    auto-connects another server.

### Root Cause

- SFTP was previously keyed by `serverID`, so same-server terminal tabs shared
  one current path, entries list, selection, transfer display, and backend SFTP
  session.
- Command panel history/favorite queries previously defaulted to the active
  server request shape, so the UI had no way to request cross-server command
  history/favorites or render their source.

### Tests Added / Updated

- Go:
  - same server can open two independent SFTP contexts;
  - closing one SFTP context does not close the sibling context;
  - server-wide SFTP stop closes the remaining context;
  - command history all/current scopes return the correct rows;
  - all-scope command history joins server names;
  - all-scope favorites return all scopes and source names.
- Frontend:
  - SFTP store context isolation for entries, paths, transfers, and close;
  - TerminalWorkspace SFTP panel follows active terminal context;
  - closing one terminal calls only `CloseSftpContext` plus `CloseTerminal`;
  - SFTP panel requests include context IDs;
  - Command panel all/current filter request shape and badges;
  - command store cache separates all-history from current-server history.

### Validation

- `gofmt`: passed.
- `go vet ./...`: passed.
- `go test ./...`: passed.
- `cmd.exe /c npm run type-check`: passed.
- `cmd.exe /c npm run test -- --run`: passed, 39 files and 256 tests.
- `cmd.exe /c npm run build`: passed with existing Vite chunk-size warning.
- `wails build`: passed for Windows amd64.

### Manual Verification Checklist

1. Open the same server and create terminal #1.
2. In terminal #1 SFTP, enter `/root`.
3. Create terminal #2 for the same server.
4. In terminal #2 SFTP, enter `/var/www`.
5. Switch back to terminal #1 and confirm SFTP still shows `/root`.
6. Switch back to terminal #2 and confirm SFTP still shows `/var/www`.
7. Upload/download in terminal #1 and confirm terminal #2's SFTP path/list is
   not overwritten.
8. Close terminal #1 and confirm terminal #2 plus its SFTP context remain
   usable.
9. Disconnect the server and confirm all terminal/SFTP contexts close.
10. Open the Command panel and confirm `全部命令 / 当前服务器` exists.
11. Confirm all mode shows multiple-server history with source badges.
12. Confirm current-server mode only shows current-server history.
13. Confirm all-mode insert/execute sends only to the active terminal.
14. Confirm favorites still add/edit/delete/use normally.
15. Confirm Local Terminal remains hidden by default.

### Risks / Not Done

- Manual real-server verification is still required for two same-server SFTP
  contexts with different remote paths.
- Pause/resume transfer, SCP remote listing, Docker/port forwarding, macOS
  Keychain, and Local Terminal re-enable remain out of scope.

## 2026-06-18: Per-Session Terminal Tabs And Compact Command List Correction

Current baseline before this correction:
`498cf1f9f4c0e1287ca0c5f1832a1fd702452f8c`.

Stage: correction for Terminal tab semantics and Command panel density after
the prior interaction pass. This is not a new feature stage.

Windows executable: `build/bin/ServerPilot.exe`

Windows executable SHA-256:
`95ADAB25397C668DD41D60C6EA98BB4A5C75CC319075864678291EAEFBCA1481`

### Scope

- Changed frontend Terminal tab rendering, Terminal tab context menu, terminal
  store close orchestration, Command panel list markup/CSS, tests, and docs.
- Did not modify SSH runtime architecture.
- Did not modify Terminal backend PTY lifecycle.
- Did not modify Monitor runtime.
- Did not modify SFTP runtime or SCP fallback runtime.
- Did not modify `DisconnectServer(serverID)` semantics.
- Did not modify workspace state model.
- Did not modify SecretStore, Key Vault, or Backup / Import / Export behavior.
- Local Terminal remains default-disabled/quarantined.

### Implemented

- Restored terminal tabs to per-session semantics:
  - tab identity uses `sessionID`;
  - same `serverID` can render multiple terminal tabs;
  - active terminal tab follows `activeTerminalSessionID`;
  - `activeWorkspaceServerID` follows the active terminal's server.
- Same-server tab labels are disambiguated as `name`, `name #2`, `name #3`.
- Terminal tab close button calls `CloseTerminal(sessionID)` only.
- Closing one terminal tab no longer calls `DisconnectServer(serverID)`.
- Closing one terminal tab does not close same-server sibling terminals, SFTP,
  Monitor, or SFTP/SCP transfers.
- Store activation now follows the adjacent remaining terminal when the active
  tab is closed, including adjacent terminals from another server.
- Terminal tab context menu now contains:
  - `仅关闭当前终端`;
  - `关闭此服务器全部终端`;
  - `断开此服务器`.
- `关闭此服务器全部终端` calls `CloseTerminal(sessionID)` for that server's
  terminal sessions only.
- `断开此服务器` is the only terminal tab menu action that emits the
  `DisconnectServer(serverID)` intent.
- Removed ambiguous visible `关闭服务器工作区` wording.
- Command panel history/favorites rows are compact single-line rows:
  - long commands ellipsize;
  - actions are shown on hover/focus;
  - favorites show title and command in one row;
  - search/tabs/list spacing is tighter.
- Command history still loads up to 200 rows by default.

### Tests Added / Updated

- WorkspaceTabs:
  - same server renders three terminal tabs for three session IDs;
  - tab DOM exposes session IDs for verification;
  - closing the second terminal tab only closes that session;
  - `仅关闭当前终端` exists on terminal tab context menu;
  - `关闭此服务器全部终端` closes only terminal sessions;
  - `断开此服务器` is the explicit server disconnect path;
  - `关闭服务器工作区` remains absent.
- Terminal store:
  - closing the active tab activates the adjacent remaining terminal;
  - closing all terminal sessions for one server does not call
    `DisconnectServer`.
- CommandPalette:
  - history rows are compact single-line rows;
  - favorite rows are compact single-line rows;
  - long command text is available through `title` and ellipsizes visually.

6. Confirm closing one tab does not disconnect SSH server state.
7. Confirm closing one tab does not disconnect SFTP.

## 2026-06-17: SCP Fallback MVP

Current baseline before this stage:
`2ab4c83d931468c33ec83f6b40e4de47aa77400f`.

Stage: SCP fallback for servers without an SFTP subsystem, including
OpenWrt / ImmortalWrt / Dropbear-style environments. This stage attaches SCP
as a compatibility transfer backend inside the existing SFTP manager. It does
not rebuild the SSH, Terminal, Monitor, Workspace, SecretStore, Key Vault, or
Backup architecture.

Windows executable: `build/bin/ServerPilot.exe`

Windows executable SHA-256:
`9E2014DADACBC6BFAAC02EDFFB6EA7034CBB02032E5A9D6328A9950519D223AB`

### Scope

- Changed SFTP manager/runtime, controlled SSH command execution, typed domain
  models, generated Wails models, SFTP frontend store/UI, transfer display,
  tests, and handoff docs.
- Did not modify SSH Terminal runtime.
- Did not modify Terminal PTY lifecycle.
- Did not modify Monitor runtime.
- Did not modify `DisconnectServer(serverID)` semantics.
- Did not modify workspace state model.
- Did not modify SecretStore, Key Vault, or CredentialResolver.
- Did not modify Backup / Import / Export behavior.
- Did not re-enable Local Terminal; it remains default-disabled/quarantined.
- SFTP/SCP transfer remains independent from terminal and monitor sessions.

### Implemented

- Domain/API
  - Added `SFTPMode` with `sftp` and `scp`.
  - Added `SFTPBrowseCapability` and `SFTPCapabilities`.
  - Added mode/capabilities to `SFTPState`.
  - Added mode to `SFTPListResult`.
  - Added mode to `SFTPTransferState`.
  - Regenerated Wails frontend models.
- Backend SFTP runtime
  - Added `Transport.StartCommand` for controlled remote commands.
  - Added `sshclient.CommandSession` with stdin/stdout/wait/close access.
  - Context cancellation closes the SSH command session.
  - SFTP open failures matching unsupported subsystem, subsystem request
    failed, remote closed during init, missing `sftp-server`, channel closed,
    EOF, or connection closed now enter SCP compatibility mode.
  - SCP fallback keeps the existing independent SFTP transport alive and marks
    the server SFTP state online with `mode=scp`.
  - SCP fallback emits a visible compatibility warning/error event.
  - SCP mode reports `browse=none`.
  - SCP mode list calls return an empty typed list and do not fake entries.
  - Browse-dependent operations return clear Chinese unsupported errors:
    stat/properties, remote text editor, write text file, mkdir, rename,
    delete preview, and delete.
- SCP protocol
  - Upload file through legacy `scp -t`.
  - Download file through legacy `scp -f`.
  - Upload directory through legacy recursive `scp -r -t`.
  - Download directory through legacy recursive `scp -r -f`.
  - Handles SCP ack bytes, warning/fatal records, `C`, `D`, `E`, and `T`
    records.
  - Transfer progress updates bytes, speed, current file, file totals where
    discoverable, and final status through the existing transfer queue.
  - Transfer cancellation uses the existing `SftpCancelTransfer` API and the
    transfer context.
- Safety
  - Rejects empty remote path, `/`, `.`, `..`, and parent traversal.
  - Quotes all shell arguments with single-quote escaping.
  - Rejects unsafe SCP record names containing path separators, traversal,
    newline, NUL, or reserved names.
  - Download writes are constrained to the selected local target directory.
  - Directory upload skips local symlinks by default and does not follow them.
  - Remote file contents, local file contents, passwords, passphrases, private
    keys, and raw SCP streams are not logged.
- Frontend
  - SFTP store normalizes missing mode/capability fields.
  - Opening SCP fallback state does not call `ReadSftpDir`.
  - SCP mode UI shows `SCP compatibility mode`.
  - SCP mode does not render the SFTP table or fake file rows.
  - SCP mode keeps explicit upload/download file and directory actions.
  - SCP mode disables refresh listing, mkdir, rename, delete, properties,
    remote editor, and normal browsing.
  - Transfer summary/status bar/popover prefixes SCP transfers with `SCP`.

### Tests Added / Updated

- Go
  - Missing SFTP subsystem falls back to SCP instead of closing the transport.
  - SCP upload file sends expected protocol records.
  - SCP download file parses expected protocol records.
  - SCP upload directory sends recursive `D`/`C`/`E` records.
  - SCP download directory parses recursive `D`/`C`/`E` records.
  - Unsafe SCP remote paths are rejected.
  - SCP ack errors are reported as visible errors.
  - Unsafe remote SCP record names are rejected.
  - Existing recursive transfer cancel and Stop behavior still cover the shared
    transfer lifecycle used by SCP.
- Frontend
  - SCP compatibility mode renders without a fake file list.
  - SCP upload uses the typed `SftpUpload` API with the entered remote path.
  - SCP download uses the typed `SftpDownload` API with the entered remote path.
  - SCP fallback open does not call `ReadSftpDir`.
  - Transfer popover/status expectations include `SCP` source text.
  - Existing SFTP icons, recursive delete, transfer, editor, Settings sticky
    save button, and Local Terminal disabled tests remain covered.

### Validation

- `gofmt`: passed.
- `go vet ./...`: passed.
- `go test ./...`: passed.
- `cmd.exe /c npm run type-check`: passed.
- `cmd.exe /c npm run test -- --run`: passed, 37 files and 232 tests.
- `cmd.exe /c npm run build`: passed with existing Vite chunk-size warning.
- `wails build`: passed for Windows amd64.

### Manual Verification Checklist

1. Test a normal Linux SFTP server and confirm it still uses SFTP mode.
2. Upload a single file in normal SFTP mode.
3. Download a single file in normal SFTP mode.
4. Confirm normal SFTP recursive upload/download still works.
5. Confirm normal SFTP recursive delete still works.
6. Test an OpenWrt/Dropbear host without SFTP subsystem.
7. Confirm UI shows SCP compatibility mode.
8. In SCP mode, input a remote path and upload a single file.
9. In SCP mode, input a remote path and download a single file.
10. In SCP mode, upload a directory.
11. In SCP mode, download a directory.
12. Cancel an SCP transfer.
13. Confirm SCP errors show Chinese toast/status.
14. Confirm SCP failure does not affect SSH terminal.
15. Confirm SCP failure does not affect Monitor.
16. Confirm `DisconnectServer` cancels that server's SCP transfer.
17. Confirm Local Terminal remains hidden by default.
18. Confirm Backup / Export does not include transfer records.

### Known Limitations

- Shell-based remote listing is not implemented in SCP mode.
- SCP mode is explicit-path transfer only.
- Remote editor, delete, rename, mkdir, properties, and full browse are
  unavailable in SCP mode.
- Pause/resume is not implemented.
- Symlink-following recursive behavior is not implemented.
- OpenWrt/Dropbear real-host validation was not performed in this environment.

## 2026-06-17: SFTP Folder Icons and Recursive Delete

Current baseline before this stage:
`31d1097da912972b6586f3a6a6e210f727f1289a`.

Stage: SFTP file-list recognizability and recursive directory delete. This
round improves the existing bottom SFTP panel only. It does not implement SCP
fallback, pause/resume, Docker/port forwarding, macOS Keychain, or any Local
Terminal re-enable path.

Windows executable: `build/bin/ServerPilot.exe`

Windows executable SHA-256:
`005A2F66B2580F3C5BD35BA832A7E30170A2B17D2597AFF0E7F71FA6A70E48B8`

### Scope

- Changed only SFTP manager, typed Wails API bindings, SFTP frontend store/UI,
  SFTP CSS, tests, and handoff docs.
- Did not modify SSH runtime.
- Did not modify Terminal runtime.
- Did not modify Monitor runtime.
- Did not modify `DisconnectServer(serverID)` semantics.
- Did not modify workspace state model.
- Did not modify SecretStore, Key Vault, or CredentialResolver.
- Did not modify Backup / Import / Export behavior.
- Did not re-enable Local Terminal; it remains default-disabled/quarantined.
- SFTP remains independent from terminal and monitor sessions.

### Implemented

- Domain/API
  - Extended `SFTPDeleteRequest` with multi-path and recursive fields while
    keeping old `path` / `isDir` compatibility.
  - Added `SFTPInspectDeleteRequest`.
  - Added `SFTPInspectDeleteResponse`.
  - Added Wails method `SftpInspectDelete`.
  - Regenerated Wails frontend bindings.
- Backend SFTP runtime
  - Added delete preview that counts files, directories, symlinks, total bytes,
    warnings, and whether recursive confirmation is required.
  - Added recursive directory deletion in `internal/sftpmanager`.
  - Recursive delete scans the existing SFTP session, deletes files/symlinks
    first, then removes directories bottom-up.
  - Symlinks are removed as links and are not followed.
  - Empty path, `/`, `.`, `..`, and parent traversal are rejected.
  - Nested multi-selected paths are collapsed so deleting a parent covers child
    selections.
  - Non-recursive delete of a non-empty directory returns a clear Chinese error:
    `目录非空，请使用递归删除`.
  - Delete failures do not close the SFTP session.
  - Delete does not call `DisconnectServer`.
- Frontend
  - SFTP list now displays distinct inline SVG icons for parent, folder, file,
    and symlink rows.
  - Directory type text changed from `目录` to `文件夹`.
  - Directory context menu no longer shows `删除空目录`.
  - Directory context menu now shows `删除目录及内容...`.
  - File context menu now shows `删除文件`.
  - Delete action snapshots selected entries, calls `SftpInspectDelete`, then
    shows the existing danger app dialog before deleting.
  - Canceling the danger dialog does not call backend delete.
  - Confirming directory or mixed delete calls `SftpDelete` with
    `recursive: true`.
  - Multi-select file + directory deletion is handled as one confirmed request.
  - Successful delete refreshes the current path only if the user is still on
    that same path.

### Tests Added / Updated

- Go
  - Delete preview counts files, directories, and symlinks.
  - Non-empty directory delete requires recursive mode.
  - Recursive delete removes nested files and directories bottom-up.
  - Recursive delete removes symlink entries without following them.
  - Empty path, `/`, `.`, `..`, and traversal paths are rejected.
  - Multi-select file + directory recursive delete works.
  - Delete failure does not close the SFTP session.
- Frontend
  - Parent/folder/file/symlink icons render.
  - Directory context menu uses recursive wording and not `删除空目录`.
  - Directory delete calls inspect first.
  - Danger dialog cancel does not call delete.
  - Danger dialog confirm calls recursive `SftpDelete`.
  - Multi-select file + directory delete uses one recursive request.
  - Existing SFTP editor, details, upload, download, and transfer tests remain
    covered.

### Validation

- `go vet ./...`: passed.
- `go test ./...`: passed.
- `cmd.exe /c npm run type-check`: passed.
- `cmd.exe /c npm run test -- --run`: passed, 37 files and 228 tests.
- `cmd.exe /c npm run build`: passed with existing Vite chunk-size warning.
- `wails build`: passed for Windows amd64.

3. Confirm symlink rows have a distinct link icon.
4. Right-click an ordinary file and confirm the menu shows `删除文件`.
5. Right-click a directory and confirm the menu no longer shows `删除空目录`.
6. Click `删除目录及内容...` for a directory.
7. Confirm a dangerous second-confirmation dialog is shown.
8. Confirm a non-empty directory can be deleted after confirmation.

13. Confirm canceling the dialog does not delete anything.

15. Confirm successful delete refreshes the current directory.
16. Confirm SSH terminal remains unaffected.
17. Confirm SFTP session remains connected after delete failure.
18. Confirm Local Terminal remains hidden by default.
19. Confirm Settings save button remains sticky/floating and visible.

## 2026-06-17: SFTP Recursive Transfer MVP

Current baseline before this stage:
`48c47a8e9faa5b6044a93110391b918e1999dec0`.

Stage: SFTP recursive directory transfer. This round adds recursive upload and
download for the existing bottom SFTP panel only. It does not implement SCP
fallback, Local Terminal History, Docker/port forwarding, macOS Keychain, or any
Local Terminal re-enable path.

Windows executable: `build/bin/ServerPilot.exe`

Windows executable SHA-256:
`7B022128512550871F2C63B509F2FEF70FE2067C78DA29FD5EDD68CC9F564E8F`

### Scope

- Changed only SFTP manager, typed Wails API bindings, SFTP frontend store/UI,
  transfer status UI, tests, and handoff docs.
- Did not modify SSH runtime.
- Did not modify Terminal runtime.
- Did not modify Monitor runtime.
- Did not modify `DisconnectServer(serverID)` semantics.
- Did not modify workspace state model.
- Did not modify SecretStore, Key Vault, or CredentialResolver.
- Did not modify Backup / Import / Export behavior.
- Did not re-enable Local Terminal; it remains default-disabled/quarantined.
- SFTP remains independent from terminal and monitor sessions.

### Implemented

- Domain/API
  - Added `SFTPUploadDirectoryRequest`.
  - Added `SFTPDownloadDirectoryRequest`.
  - Added transfer statuses `planning` and `partial_failed`.
  - Extended `SFTPTransferState` with recursive metadata:
    `recursive`, `sourceType`, `currentFile`, `filesTotal`, `filesDone`,
    `failedCount`, `skippedCount`, and `cancelable`.
  - Added Wails methods:
    `SftpUploadDirectory`, `SftpDownloadDirectory`,
    `SelectLocalUploadDirectory`.
  - Regenerated Wails frontend bindings.
- Backend SFTP runtime
  - `Manager.Upload()` now detects local directories and delegates to recursive
    upload.
  - Added `Manager.UploadDirectory()` and `Manager.DownloadDirectory()`.
  - Added recursive planning helpers for upload/download.
  - Local path handling uses `filepath`; remote path handling keeps POSIX
    semantics.
  - Windows local relative paths are converted safely to POSIX remote paths.
  - Remote `..` traversal and local target escape are rejected.
  - Symlinks are skipped by default and never followed.
  - Recursive upload creates remote directories and streams files one by one.
  - Recursive download creates local directories and streams files one by one.
  - Recursive jobs update current file, file counts, total bytes, speed,
    skipped count, and failed count.
  - Recursive cancel uses only the transfer context and does not call
    `DisconnectServer`.
  - Recursive failure marks the transfer failed/partial-failed without closing
    the SFTP session.
  - `Stop(connectionID)` cancels queued/planning/running transfers for that
    server before closing the SFTP runtime.
- Frontend
  - Added `uploadDirectory` store action.
  - `downloadEntries` now calls recursive directory download for selected
    directories and normal file download for selected files.
  - Added `上传文件夹` entry in the SFTP panel.
  - Added `下载目录` directory context-menu entry.
  - Mixed file + directory download works from current selection.
  - Recursive `ask` conflict policy is resolved once before the job as
    overwrite, rename, or skip.
  - Status bar and transfer popover show directory transfer identity, current
    file, file count progress, byte progress, skipped/failed counts, and cancel.
  - `partial_failed` is treated as a terminal transfer state for UI cleanup.

### Tests Added / Updated

- Go
  - Recursive upload plan preserves directory structure.
  - Recursive upload creates remote directories and uploads multiple files.
  - Recursive upload skips symlinks.
  - Recursive upload conflict overwrite and skip.
  - Recursive download creates local directories and downloads multiple files.
  - Recursive download skips symlinks.
  - Recursive transfer cancel does not close the SFTP session.
  - Recursive failure does not close the SFTP session.
  - `Stop(connectionID)` cancels recursive transfers for that server.
  - Traversal and local target escape are rejected.
  - Windows local path segments convert to POSIX remote paths.
- Frontend
  - Directory download and upload-folder UI entries exist.
  - Selected local folder upload calls the directory API.
  - Mixed file + directory selection downloads recursively for directories.
  - Dropped folder path is accepted through the upload path.
  - Transfer summary shows directory/current file/file count.
  - Status bar/popover show recursive details and cancel.
  - `partial_failed` recursive transfers can be cleared as terminal records.

### Validation

- `gofmt`: passed.
- `go vet ./...`: passed.
- `go test ./...`: passed.
- `cmd.exe /c npm run type-check`: passed.
- `cmd.exe /c npm run test -- --run`: passed, 37 files and 223 tests.
- `cmd.exe /c npm run build`: passed with existing Vite chunk-size warning.
- `wails build`: passed for Windows amd64.

### Manual Verification Checklist

1. Upload a folder with multiple nested subdirectories.
2. Upload a folder containing Chinese filenames and filenames with spaces.
3. Upload with overwrite conflict policy.
4. Upload with skip conflict policy.
5. Cancel during upload.
6. Confirm completed upload refreshes the current remote directory when still
   viewing the target directory.
7. Download one remote directory.
8. Download a mixed selection: file + directory.
9. Cancel during download.
10. Confirm symlink entries are skipped.
11. Confirm transfer popover shows file-count progress.
12. Confirm status bar shows the current file.
13. Confirm SSH terminal is unaffected.
14. Confirm SFTP session does not disconnect after recursive cancel/failure.
15. Confirm Local Terminal remains hidden by default.
16. Confirm Settings save button remains sticky/floating and visible.

### Remaining / Not Implemented

- SCP fallback.
- Pause/resume transfer.
- Per-file conflict modal during recursive jobs.
- Symlink following.
- Stable Windows Local Terminal rewrite/proof.

- Did not modify SSH runtime.

## 2026-06-17: Quarantine Unstable Local Terminal Runtime

Current baseline before this stage:
`71225aaeb90dc9713b11119f28bd5a78e20a98cd`.

Stage: emergency isolation for Local Terminal after real-machine retest still
showed app exit/crash on tab close or shell `exit`, plus blank/no-input local
terminal surface.

Windows executable: `build/bin/ServerPilot.exe`

Windows executable SHA-256:
`9E0741CAB9F5A297DF289BEB6B0A91765C41A3E1FFCBB6B92CA31CD7B8979DF7`

### Final Decision

- Local Terminal is quarantined and disabled by default.
- This is the accepted result B from the user request: unstable Local Terminal
  is no longer exposed to normal users.
- Temporary development-only opt-in:
  `SERVERPILOT_LOCAL_TERMINAL_EXPERIMENTAL=1`.
- The feature must not be re-enabled by default until a real Windows GUI smoke
  proves output, input, shell exit, tab close, and no external console windows.

### Scope

- Did not modify SSH runtime architecture.
- Did not modify SSH terminal PTY lifecycle.
- Did not modify Monitor runtime.
- Did not modify SFTP runtime.
- Did not modify `DisconnectServer(serverID)`.
- Did not modify workspace state model.
- Did not modify SecretStore, Key Vault, CredentialResolver, Backup / Import /
  Export, or command history/favorites/completion models.

### Diagnosis

- The previous close-path repair did not solve the user's real-machine crash,
  so the existing Local Terminal implementation cannot be considered stable.
- Hard audit found no direct app-level path from `CloseLocalTerminal` or shell
  exit into `DisconnectServer`, `CloseAll`, `runtime.Quit`, `WindowClose`, or
  `os.Exit`.
- Windows adapter source contains no `GenerateConsoleCtrlEvent`,
  `CTRL_C_EVENT`, or `CTRL_BREAK_EVENT`.
- Windows adapter still uses `CREATE_NO_WINDOW`, `CREATE_NEW_PROCESS_GROUP`,
  and `HideWindow`.
- The blank/no-input behavior was not proven fixed. Treat the current
  third-party PTY path as unstable under real Wails GUI conditions.

### Implemented

- Added Local Terminal gate:
  - `localterminal.ExperimentalEnv`;
  - `localterminal.ExperimentalEnabled`;
  - `localterminal.ErrDisabled`;
  - `domain.LocalTerminalCapabilities.Enabled`.
- Backend API behavior while disabled:
  - `OpenLocalTerminal` returns `LOCAL_TERMINAL_DISABLED` before touching the
    manager;
  - no session, PTY, process, or shell is created;
  - `WriteLocalTerminal` and `ResizeLocalTerminal` return disabled;
  - `CloseLocalTerminal` returns nil safely;
  - `ListLocalTerminals` returns empty list.
- Frontend behavior while disabled:
  - app startup reads backend capabilities;
  - local terminal store defaults to disabled;
  - late `localterminal:output` and `localterminal:state` events are ignored;
  - stale local tabs can be removed without backend calls;
  - `+` server menu hides Local Terminal;
  - Settings hides Local Terminal shell preference.
- Kept experimental path:
  - when env var is `1`, UI entry and settings can appear;
  - existing LocalTerminalView/store tests still cover output/input plumbing.
- Added Windows adapter static safety test rejecting console-control event usage.

### Files Changed

- `app.go`
- `app_test.go`
- `internal/domain/localterminal.go`
- `internal/localterminal/manager.go`
- `internal/localterminal/manager_test.go`
- `internal/localterminal/process_windows_test.go`
- `frontend/src/App.vue`
- `frontend/src/stores/localTerminal.ts`
- `frontend/src/components/ServerPicker.vue`
- `frontend/src/components/SettingsView.vue`
- `frontend/src/types.ts`
- `frontend/wailsjs/go/models.ts`
- frontend tests for App, ServerPicker, SettingsView, LocalTerminalView,
  WorkspaceTabs, and localTerminal store
- `DEV_PROGRESS.md`
- `AI_BRIEF.md`
- `HANDOFF.md`

- `go vet ./...`: passed.
- `go test ./...`: passed.
- `cmd.exe /c npm run type-check`: passed.
- `cmd.exe /c npm run test -- --run`: 37 files, 216 tests passed.
- `cmd.exe /c npm run build`: passed with existing Vite chunk-size warning.
- `wails build`: passed for Windows amd64.

- After close, process checks found `0` local-terminal shell processes.
