## 2026-06-17: Local Terminal Close Lifecycle And Topbar Entry Fix

Current baseline before this stage:
`60ba9eb75a90b2e4225e1abe59cbd59eee72a101`.

Stage: regression repair for Local Terminal tab close / shell exit causing
ServerPilot to close or crash, plus removal of the permanent topbar Local
Terminal shortcut.

Windows executable: `build/bin/ServerPilot.exe`

Windows executable SHA-256:
`B3C36BD1BBA88CEDEC3907895E0301A43B412F1785FA8084C21B3BCFEB7867D4`

### Scope

- Fixed regressions only.
- Did not modify SSH runtime architecture, Terminal SSH PTY lifecycle, Monitor
  runtime, SFTP runtime, `DisconnectServer`, workspace state model,
  SecretStore, Key Vault, CredentialResolver, or Backup / Import / Export.
- Local Terminal remains an independent runtime, not serverID-based and not
  part of `DisconnectServer`.
- Did not implement SCP fallback, recursive transfer, Local Terminal History,
  Docker, port forwarding, macOS Keychain, or any new workspace model.

### Root Causes

- Backend audit found no direct `CloseLocalTerminal -> DisconnectServer`,
  `CloseLocalTerminal -> CloseAll`, `CloseLocalTerminal -> runtime.Quit`, or
  `CloseLocalTerminal -> app shutdown` path.
- The remaining backend crash path was the Local Terminal event boundary:
  emitter panics during `localterminal:output`, `localterminal:state`, or
  `localterminal:error` were not isolated. Shell exit emits a final state event,
  so an event-layer panic there could terminate the process.
- The tab close control used stop propagation but did not prevent default
  pointer/mouse/click behavior. In the topbar tab structure, that left the close
  action insufficiently isolated from tab activation/default behavior.
- Closing an active Local Terminal tab had no adjacent-tab fallback, so the UI
  could land in an ambiguous empty active state even when server tabs existed.
- `WorkspaceTabs` still exposed a permanent `本地终端` shortcut even though the
  `+` menu already contained the correct entry.

### Implemented

- Wrapped Local Terminal event dispatch in `safeEmit`; emitter panics are
  recovered and logged as sanitized local terminal event-send failures.
- Removed the permanent topbar Local Terminal shortcut from `WorkspaceTabs`.
- Removed unused `open-local-terminal` event bindings from `WorkspaceTabs`
  instances in `App.vue`.
- Kept the Local Terminal open entry in `ServerPicker` under the top `+` menu.
- Changed Local Terminal tab close handlers to:
  - `pointerdown.stop.prevent`;
  - `mousedown.stop.prevent`;
  - `click.stop.prevent`.
- Added close fallback so closing the active local tab activates an adjacent
  SSH workspace/local tab when one exists, or leaves an empty state if no tabs
  remain.
- Added backend lifecycle tests:
  - close one local terminal does not cancel manager root context;
  - shell exit does not cancel manager root context;
  - close one local terminal does not affect another;
  - emitter panic does not escape Local Terminal lifecycle.
- Added frontend tests:
  - `+` menu still contains Local Terminal entry;
  - topbar permanent Local Terminal shortcut is absent;
  - local tab close calls `CloseLocalTerminal`;
  - local tab close does not emit `disconnectServer`;
  - local tab close events are prevented and do not bubble into tab activation;
  - closing active local tab falls back to adjacent server workspace.

### Files Changed

- `internal/localterminal/manager.go`
- `internal/localterminal/manager_test.go`
- `frontend/src/components/WorkspaceTabs.vue`
- `frontend/src/components/WorkspaceTabs.test.ts`
- `frontend/src/components/ServerPicker.test.ts`
- `frontend/src/App.vue`
- `frontend/src/style.css`
- `DEV_PROGRESS.md`
- `AI_BRIEF.md`
- `HANDOFF.md`

### Automated Validation

- `gofmt`: passed.
- `go vet ./...`: passed.
- `go test ./...`: passed.
- `cmd.exe /c npm run type-check`: passed.
- `cmd.exe /c npm run test -- --run`: 37 files, 211 tests passed.
- `cmd.exe /c npm run build`: passed with existing Vite chunk-size warning.
- `wails build`: passed for Windows amd64.

### User Should Test

1. Open Local Terminal from the top `+` menu.
2. Confirm the topbar no longer has a permanent standalone `本地终端` button.
3. Click Local Terminal once; confirm only one local terminal tab opens.
4. In PowerShell, run `exit`; ServerPilot must remain open.
5. In cmd, run `exit`; ServerPilot must remain open.
6. Click the Local Terminal tab close control; ServerPilot must remain open.
7. Confirm SSH terminal remains online.
8. Confirm SFTP remains online.
9. Confirm Monitor remains online.
10. Confirm closing Local Terminal does not call `DisconnectServer`.
11. Confirm `DisconnectServer(serverID)` does not close Local Terminal.
12. Confirm normal app exit closes Local Terminal sessions.

### Remaining Risks

- I did not launch the GUI and reproduce the exact user-machine crash in this
  turn. The fix is based on code-path audit, targeted lifecycle tests, full
  automated validation, and production build validation.
- If an OS-level Windows console control event outside Go panic/error handling
  still terminates the GUI process, the next step is an instrumented manual run
  that records the process tree and child process exit path.

### Still Not Done

- SCP fallback.
- Recursive directory transfer.
- Local Terminal History.
- Docker / port forwarding.
- macOS Keychain native implementation.

## 2026-06-17: SSH Unicode And Local Terminal Process Isolation Fixes

Current baseline before this stage:
`924f485be9fe4984c8ef069f3febe8afbf67eb05`.

Stage: real-machine regression repair for four issues still failing after the
previous round: Debian Oh My Zsh Chinese input, Windows Local Terminal external
console/process isolation, compact monitor collapsed restore splitter, and
terminal bottom black strip.

Windows executable: `build/bin/ServerPilot.exe`

Windows executable SHA-256:
`072C21F93F2B861CF6B8B956BA20F1DBABA8A0997E39C8BAADC90512EA169D70`

### Scope

- Fixed regressions only.
- Did not modify SSH runtime architecture, Monitor runtime, SFTP runtime,
  `DisconnectServer`, workspace model, SecretStore, Key Vault,
  CredentialResolver, or Backup / Import / Export secret behavior.
- Did not implement SCP fallback, recursive transfer, Local Terminal History,
  Docker, port forwarding, macOS Keychain, or any new workspace model.

### Root Causes

- The previous SSH Unicode fix only proved the frontend encoder. The real
  remote terminal could still start under a non-UTF-8 locale, causing Oh My Zsh
  ZLE to render valid multi-byte input as invalid `<ffffffff>` cells.
- The Local Terminal Windows command path did not explicitly apply hidden
  ConPTY process flags or isolate the child process group.
- Local Terminal read/write/wait goroutines had no panic boundary, so a PTY or
  child process panic could escape and terminate the app.
- Compact monitor collapsed mode removed the splitter row entirely.
- Xterm helper/root/leftover pixel backgrounds were not all forced to the same
  terminal surface.

### Implemented

- SSH terminal startup now attempts UTF-8 env setup with `Setenv` before shell
  start: `LC_CTYPE=C.UTF-8`, `LANG=C.UTF-8`, then `en_US.UTF-8` fallback.
  Setenv failure does not block terminal startup and no shell `export` command
  is written to the terminal.
- Added end-to-end byte tests for `啊`:
  frontend wrapper `dataBase64` -> `App.WriteTerminal` -> terminal manager ->
  mock shell receives `E5 95 8A`.
- Added Windows Local Terminal process setup:
  `CREATE_NO_WINDOW`, `CREATE_NEW_PROCESS_GROUP`, and `HideWindow`.
- Added Local Terminal recover guards for read/write/wait paths with sanitized
  local terminal state/error events.
- Restored visible collapsed monitor restore splitters:
  - `monitorCollapsed`: 10 px restore splitter + mount pane;
  - `mountsCollapsed`: monitor pane + 10 px restore splitter.
- Terminal surface CSS now forces `--terminal-bg` across xterm root, screen,
  viewport, rows, helper textarea, and the bottom leftover pixel.

### Files Changed

- `app_test.go`
- `internal/sshclient/terminal.go`
- `internal/sshclient/terminal_test.go`
- `internal/terminal/manager_test.go`
- `internal/localterminal/manager.go`
- `internal/localterminal/manager_test.go`
- `internal/localterminal/process_windows.go`
- `internal/localterminal/process_other.go`
- `internal/localterminal/process_windows_test.go`
- `frontend/src/api/backend.test.ts`
- `frontend/src/components/CompactMonitorSidebar.vue`
- `frontend/src/components/CompactMonitorSidebar.test.ts`
- `frontend/src/style.css`
- `frontend/src/style.tokens.test.ts`
- `DEV_PROGRESS.md`
- `AI_BRIEF.md`
- `HANDOFF.md`

### Automated Validation

- `gofmt`: passed.
- `go vet ./...`: passed.
- `go test ./...`: passed.
- `cmd.exe /c npm run type-check`: passed.
- `cmd.exe /c npm run test -- --run`: 37 files, 208 tests passed.
- `cmd.exe /c npm run build`: passed with existing Vite chunk-size warning.
- `wails build`: passed for Windows amd64.

### User Should Test

1. Debian Oh My Zsh: type `啊`; confirm it displays as `啊`.
2. Debian Oh My Zsh: run `echo 中文测试`; confirm output is correct.
3. Bash: run `echo 中文测试`.
4. Confirm Ctrl+C, Ctrl+Space completion, Tab, vim/top/nano/htop still work.
5. Open Local Terminal with cmd preference; confirm no external cmd window.
6. Open Local Terminal with PowerShell preference; confirm no external
   PowerShell window.
7. Close Local Terminal tab and let shell exit; ServerPilot must remain open.

## 2026-06-17: Terminal Unicode, Local Tabs, And Collapsed Layout Fixes

Current baseline before this stage:
`a65e979354e372755eae0057e4b722e7f62712af`.

Stage: real-machine regression repair for SSH Unicode input, Local Terminal
tab lifecycle, SFTP collapsed layout, compact monitor collapsed layout,
terminal surface visuals, and Local Terminal shell preference.

Windows executable: `build/bin/ServerPilot.exe`

Windows executable SHA-256:
`6263D64B2A22FBB98BC742069B1D22534BCE82E437E7A17AE9142EBDADD7611E`

### Scope

- Fixed regressions only.
- Did not modify SSH runtime architecture, Terminal backend PTY lifecycle,
  Monitor runtime, SFTP runtime, `DisconnectServer`, workspace state model,
  SecretStore, Key Vault, CredentialResolver, or Backup / Import / Export
  secret behavior.
- Did not implement SCP fallback, recursive transfer, Local Terminal History,
  Docker, port forwarding, macOS Keychain, or any new SSH runtime.

### Implemented

- Added `frontend/src/utils/terminalEncoding.ts` and routed SSH terminal,
  local terminal, and command insertion payloads through
  `TextEncoder` UTF-8 bytes before base64.
- Added byte-level Chinese regression coverage:
  `xterm.onData("啊")` must send `E5 95 8A`, never `0xff`.
- Added IME composition tracking to SSH and local terminal key handlers so
  composing input is not intercepted by completion or shortcut logic.
- Added Local Terminal open guards at App/store levels and sessionID upsert for
  `localterminal:state`, preventing duplicate tabs from API response plus
  backend state event races.
- Local Terminal close remains isolated to `CloseLocalTerminal`; tests assert
  it does not call `DisconnectServer`.
- Added Local Terminal shell preference:
  - Settings model version `4`;
  - SQLite migration version `7`;
  - typed `GetLocalTerminalCapabilities()`;
  - Windows auto / PowerShell / cmd options;
  - macOS auto / zsh / bash capabilities.
- Windows PowerShell preference resolves `pwsh.exe` first, then
  `powershell.exe`; cmd preference resolves only `cmd.exe`.
- SFTP collapsed state now uses a zero-height panel row and renders no
  collapsed header/title/status text.
- Compact monitor collapsed mode now renders only the visible pane; monitor
  collapsed mode gives the mount list full sidebar height.
- Terminal visual surface now uses continuous `--terminal-bg` on host, xterm,
  screen, viewport, and rows with no fake `.xterm` padding.

## Current State - SFTP Editor Polish

- Date: 2026-06-16
- Branch: `master`
- Reliable baseline before this stage:
  `4f4eb714126dafaf9a06b00978326590613776a3`
- Safety tag: `checkpoint-before-sftp-editor-polish`
- Stage: SFTP editor enhancement and upload refresh fix
- Database migration version: `5` (unchanged)
- Settings model version: `3` (unchanged)
- Windows executable: `build/bin/ServerPilot.exe`
- Windows executable SHA-256:
  `037EBCF5FFB3000BF430E23A113F58A1FDA88EC3AA47307D7B7DBBF43990D898`

This stage keeps the formal bottom SFTP architecture intact. It does not
implement SCP fallback, backup/import/export, command history, command
favorites, command completion, local terminal, recursive directory transfer,
pause/resume transfer, or macOS Keychain.

## Implemented In This Stage

- Replaced the remote text editor textarea with CodeMirror 6.
- Added editor line numbers, active-line highlight, undo/redo, tab indentation,
  theme-aware dark/light colors, and language detection for common script,
  config, web, Markdown, Python, Go, YAML, JSON, TOML, XML, and plain-text
  files.
- Added Ctrl+F editor search with next/previous, case sensitivity, match count,
  no-match state, and Esc-to-close behavior.
- Added Ctrl+S save through the existing `SftpWriteTextFile` API.
- Added an obvious `×` close button and dirty-close app dialog with Save,
  Discard, and Cancel choices. Save failure keeps the editor open.
- Fixed upload completion refresh. Completed upload transfer events now schedule
  a short debounced directory refresh keyed by server and remote parent path.
  If the user is still viewing that path, the listing refreshes and selects the
  final uploaded file path when present. If the user switched directory or
  server, the UI is not pulled back.
- Refresh failure after a completed upload keeps the transfer completed and
  shows a sanitized UI error.
- Kept SFTP-less OpenWrt/ImmortalWrt behavior as an SFTP compatibility error.
  SCP fallback remains a future independent transfer path.
- Tightened the SFTP splitter toggle: the handle is centered, has clear
  expanded/collapsed direction, and consumes pointer events so dragging and
  clicking do not cross-trigger.

## Real Checks Still Required

Use a real normal Linux SFTP server:

1. Drag one and multiple files from Windows Explorer into the current SFTP
   directory and confirm the list refreshes once after completion.
2. Upload a file with Chinese characters and spaces in its name and confirm the
   final remote name appears after refresh.
3. Upload with automatic rename conflict policy and confirm the final renamed
   path is selected.
4. Start an upload, switch directory/server before completion, and confirm the
   UI is not pulled back to the original path/server.
5. Open `.conf`, `.sh`, `.json`, `.py`, `.go`, `.yml`, and unknown extension
   text files and confirm language mode and highlighting are reasonable.
6. Modify a remote text file, use Ctrl+S, then confirm the list refreshes and
   the safe size/mtime conflict checks still work.
7. Modify a file, press the close `×`, and verify Save, Discard, and Cancel
   behave correctly.
8. Use Ctrl+F search, next/previous, case sensitivity, no-match state, and Esc.
9. Confirm file contents, search keywords, passwords, private keys, and
   passphrases do not appear in logs or SQLite.
10. Re-test an OpenWrt/ImmortalWrt host without SFTP subsystem and confirm SSH
    terminal still works while SFTP remains unsupported.

- Safety tag: `checkpoint-before-sftp-compat-edit-dnd`
- Stage: SFTP compatibility, properties, remote editing, and drag/drop upload
- Database migration version: `5` (unchanged)
- Settings model version: `3` (unchanged)

This stage keeps the formal bottom SFTP architecture intact. SFTP remains an
independent SSH transport/session and is not reused from terminal or monitor
SSH sessions. It does not implement backup/import/export, command history,
command favorites, command completion, local terminal, SCP fallback, recursive
directory transfer, pause/resume transfer, or macOS Keychain.

## Implemented In This Stage

- SFTP open failures caused by missing SFTP subsystem are classified as
  SFTP-only compatibility errors. Common OpenWrt/ImmortalWrt Dropbear failure
  strings produce a Chinese message explaining that SSH terminal can still work
  and suggesting `opkg update && opkg install openssh-sftp-server`.
- The SFTP collapse/expand control moved into the horizontal splitter. Dragging
  changes height; clicking the handle toggles expansion.
- Right-click "文件属性" now selects the target and opens the right-side
  metadata/details panel instead of doing nothing.
- The metadata panel now includes hidden-file status, symlink status, and raw
  permission text in addition to existing name/type/path/size/owner/group/time
  data.
- Double-clicking a regular file opens a remote text editor. The backend
  rejects directories, oversized files, binary-looking content, and non-UTF-8
  content.
- Remote text save uses explicit SFTP APIs with expected size and mtime checks
  before replacing the remote file through a temporary file and rename.
- Dragging files from Windows Explorer into the SFTP content area uploads them
  through Wails v2 file-drop absolute paths and the existing transfer queue.
- File contents still do not cross Wails JSON for upload/download transfer
  streams; the remote text editor is the explicit, bounded exception.

  `34115` had no listeners.

## Real Checks Still Required

Use a real SSH/SFTP server and existing saved credentials:

1. Open an OpenWrt/ImmortalWrt Dropbear host without SFTP subsystem and confirm
   SFTP shows the compatibility error while SSH terminal remains usable.
2. Open a normal SFTP server and confirm saved password, local private key,
   saved Key Vault passphrase, and
   unsaved Key Vault passphrase flows still work.
3. Confirm the SFTP splitter handle expands and collapses the bottom panel, and
   dragging the splitter adjusts height without toggling.
4. Right-click a file and directory, choose "文件属性", and confirm the details
   panel opens with metadata.
5. Double-click a small UTF-8 remote text file, edit it, save it, reload the
   directory, and confirm remote content changed.
6. Try opening a binary file, directory, and large file; confirm each is refused
   with a Chinese error.
7. Drag one or more files from Windows Explorer into the SFTP file list and
   confirm upload tasks are queued. Dragging a folder should be rejected by the
   backend.
8. Upload and download small files and confirm the status bar shows the latest
   transfer.
9. Open the transfer popover, cancel a running or queued transfer, and clear
   finished records.
10. Switch between two server workspaces and confirm SFTP directories,
   selections, and transfers remain isolated.
11. Collapse and expand the SFTP panel and right-side details panel without
    losing terminal input or recreating SSH/SFTP sessions.
12. Close the server workspace and confirm `DisconnectServer` closes SFTP and
    cancels or finishes transfers.
13. Confirm logs and SQLite contain no passwords, private-key contents,
    passphrases, uploaded/downloaded file contents, or remote editor contents.

## Known Limits

- Recursive directory upload/download remains deferred.
- Transfer pause/resume remains deferred.
- SCP fallback for SFTP-less hosts remains deferred.
- Remote editing is limited to small UTF-8 text files.
- Automatic SFTP can reuse transient auth only within the current in-memory
  connection flow. If the user later reconnects without reusable or saved
  credentials, the panel asks for credentials instead of opening dialogs
  automatically.

## Suggested Next Phase

Choose the next phase after real-server SFTP UI testing. Current candidates
are backup/import/export or command history. Keep command favorites, command
completion, local terminal, and macOS Keychain for later phases.

## 2026-06-16: SFTP Save Failure And Splitter Visual Fix

Baseline: `e3fbac721b4a0e7b05617a2bd3186af0f0045e25`.

Safety checkpoint tag: `checkpoint-before-sftp-save-and-splitter-fix`.

Root cause addressed:

- The previous text-save path relied on ordinary SFTP `Rename` after writing a
  temp file. Many SFTP servers do not overwrite an existing target with normal
  rename, so a common edit-save flow could fail after the temp file was written.
- The previous mtime check compared RFC3339Nano strings directly, which could
  misclassify servers that only report second-level SFTP mtimes.
- The frontend flattened all save failures into a generic SFTP error and did
  not expose the failed backend stage.

Implemented:

- `SFTPWriteTextFileRequest.forceOverwrite` for explicit overwrite retries.
- Staged save errors with `code`, `stage`, `userMessage`,
  `technicalMessage`, `remotePath`, `operation`, and `retryable`.
- Same-directory `.serverpilot-save-<basename>-<random>.tmp` writes.
- Best-effort temp-file chmod from original permission bits.
- Replacement order: OpenSSH POSIX rename, normal SFTP rename, then
  `OpenFile(O_WRONLY|O_TRUNC)` direct-write fallback without deleting the
  original target first.
- Conflict detection using expected size and UTC Unix-second mtime.
- Frontend conflict confirmation before retrying with `forceOverwrite: true`.
- Save failures keep the editor open, preserve the dirty buffer, and show
  expandable technical details.
- Splitter chevrons now use `splitter-handle-inline` with no border,
  background block, capsule shell, or shadow.
- CodeMirror editor colors were brightened with a ServerPilot-specific token
  highlight theme.

## 2026-06-17: SFTP Toolbar, Properties, And Toast Width Polish

Baseline: `afc79defb9f57a920dadb21962f34c60c2ae51ee`.

Safety checkpoint tag: `checkpoint-before-sftp-toolbar-properties-toast-width`.

Scope:

- UI polish only for the bottom SFTP toolbar, SFTP properties action, and Toast
  width.
- No changes to `SftpWriteTextFile`, SFTP save backend flow, formal SFTP
  transport, upload/download streaming, CredentialResolver, Key Vault, SSH
  terminal lifecycle, CodeMirror core behavior, SCP fallback, or macOS
  Keychain.

Implemented:

- Removed the redundant SFTP toolbar title/status block. In expanded mode the
  toolbar now starts directly at `Home`; server and SFTP state remain in the
  content empty/error state and right-side status bar.
- Simplified the collapsed SFTP header so it no longer repeats server name,
  "未绑定服务器", or connection status text.
- Fixed the file-properties no-response bug. Root cause: missing
  `serverpilot.sftpDetailsWidth` was parsed with `Number(null)`, which becomes
  `0`; opening properties could set `detailsCollapsed = false` while the
  details column stayed effectively invisible. Details width now distinguishes
  a missing key from zero, clamps into `240..320`, and restores the default
  `280px` when opening properties.
- Added properties to the multi-select context menu. File, directory, and
  multi-select properties now select or preserve the target selection and
  expand the details panel without entering directories, opening the editor,
  downloading, refreshing, or fetching file contents.
- Kept the synthetic `..` parent row limited to parent navigation; it does not
  expose file properties.
- Made Toast cards content-adaptive with `width: fit-content`,
  `min-width: 160px`, viewport-bounded max width, normal wrapping, and
  `overflow-wrap: anywhere`. The global top-layer z-index contract is
  unchanged.

## 2026-06-17: Backup Import And Export

Baseline: `9ff630ea4fa90100900525085cce89f6f4fbb0aa`, including the short
`AI_BRIEF.md` handoff file. Functional baseline still includes
`7ba8e1f0c31b621ebd3f3a05d6105fbac81359aa`.

Safety checkpoint tag: `checkpoint-before-backup-import-export`.

Scope:

- Implement Settings -> Backup and Restore.
- Keep SSH terminal lifecycle, SFTP transport/save backend, CredentialResolver,
  Key Vault storage semantics, Windows Credential Manager, CodeMirror behavior,
  SCP fallback, command history, local terminal, and macOS Keychain unchanged.

Database and settings versions:

- Database migration version remains `5`.
- Settings model version remains `3`.
- Backup format envelope version is `1`.
- Encrypted payload schema version is `1`.

Implemented:

- `internal/backup.Service` with typed export, inspect, and import flows.
- `.spbackup` JSON envelope using Argon2id and XChaCha20-Poly1305.
- Default KDF parameters: time `3`, memory `65536 KiB`, parallelism `2`,
  random 32-byte salt.
- XChaCha20-Poly1305 with random nonce and 32-byte derived key.
- KDF parameter limits on import to prevent malicious CPU/memory abuse.
- Encrypted payload contains settings, groups, non-sensitive connection
  metadata, Key Vault metadata, and host trust metadata.
- Backup never exports saved passwords, private-key passphrases, private-key
  contents, SecretStore refs, credential refs, logs, terminal state, SFTP
  transfer records, remote editor contents, or active workspaces.
- `InspectBackup` returns only counts, warnings, conflict summaries, and the
  credential non-import notice.
- Import is transaction-backed. Existing data is not deleted or overwritten.
- Group IDs and Key Vault IDs are regenerated; connections remap to the new
  IDs.
- Name conflicts get an `(导入 N)` suffix.
- Duplicate Key Vault private-key paths are skipped.
- Connections referencing a skipped/unmapped Key Vault entry are skipped with a
  warning.
- Imported credential state is cleared: password servers show no saved
  password, and Key Vault entries have `passphrase_saved = false`.
- Host-key trust import is optional and disabled by default.
- Settings page now includes "备份与恢复" with export and import forms, password
  validation, preview, import options, and result statistics.
- Import completion refreshes settings and server/group lists.
- Wails API additions:
  `SelectBackupExportPath`, `SelectBackupImportFile`, `ExportBackup`,
  `InspectBackup`, `ImportBackup`.
- `AI_BRIEF.md` is overwritten for this current state.

## 2026-06-17: Architecture Freeze v1.0

Current baseline: `f156c8997c98658ff21360212cf38a8181885c6d`.

Scope:

- Documentation-only architecture freeze.
- No Go runtime, frontend behavior, database migration, settings model, build
  output, or generated Wails binding changes.

Frozen rules:

- SSH, Monitor, Terminal, and SFTP runtimes remain independent.
- Workspace state model remains separated from selected-server and terminal
  session state.
- `DisconnectServer(serverID)` remains the single server-wide shutdown entry.
- `SecretStore` remains the platform sensitive-storage boundary.
- Key Vault remains metadata-only.
- SQLite remains non-sensitive storage only.
- New features must attach to existing managers, stores, typed Wails APIs,
  events, or UI panels; they must not rebuild core structure.
- Runtime state and events must be isolated by `serverID`; terminal streams
  also require `sessionID`.

- `AI_BRIEF.md`
- `HANDOFF.md`

Validation:

- Documentation-only change; no build or test run required.

Next phase:

- Command history and favorites remain the recommended next implementation
  phase, under the freeze rule: attach capability, do not rebuild structure.

## 2026-06-17: Backup Import UI Disconnect Fix

Current baseline before this fix:
`9b353ff591a7ac8860663949a94ccdad0ea7fe62`.

Scope:

- Fixed Settings -> Backup and Restore import UI behavior only.
- No SSH, Monitor, Terminal, SFTP, workspace, `DisconnectServer`,
  `SecretStore`, Key Vault, Credential Resolver, or database architecture
  changes.

Root cause:

- The import button was disabled while `importPreview` was empty:
  `:disabled="importBusy || !importPreview"`.
- Selecting a `.spbackup` file stored a path, but direct import was blocked
  unless verification had already produced a preview.
- The import handler also tried to run verification as a gate, which mixed
  verify and import concerns and made the actual `ImportBackup` call unreliable
  from the user's perspective.

Fix:

- Import is now disabled only while an import is already running.
- Verify Backup and Import Backup now read the same trimmed `importPath`
  snapshot.
- `importBackup()` validates that a file path and password are present.
- After confirmation, `importBackup()` directly awaits the typed backend
  `ImportBackup` API with `{ path, password, options }`.
- Verification remains available but no longer gates import.
- Visible Chinese errors are shown for missing file, missing password, wrong
  password, damaged/tampered backup files, decrypt/read failures, and rollback
  failures.
- Console trace logs were added for the real UI call chain: file selected,
  verify clicked, import clicked, blocked guards, backend called, backend
  response, cancellation, and backend error. Logs include path and result
  counters/options only; they do not print backup passwords or file contents.

Regression tests added:

- Direct import after selecting a file and entering a password calls
  `api.importBackup` without requiring `api.inspectBackup`.
- Direct import emits console trace logs for file selected, import clicked,
  backend called, and backend response.
- Import click with no selected file shows `请选择备份文件` and does not call
  the backend.
- Import click with selected file but no password shows `请输入备份密码` and does
  not call the backend.
- Backend rollback errors show `导入失败（已回滚）`.

Validation:

- `cmd.exe /c npm run type-check`: passed.
- `cmd.exe /c npm run test -- --run SettingsView`: 12 tests passed.
- `go test ./...`: passed.
- `cmd.exe /c npm run test -- --run`: 31 files, 179 tests passed.
- `cmd.exe /c npm run build`: passed.
- `wails build`: passed for Windows amd64.

Windows executable:

- Path: `build/bin/ServerPilot.exe`
- SHA-256: `DEEF93A4877368DB0C74D33C21D482F83B3536EFDA995C926E0F473AA0464493`

Recommended user check:

1. Settings -> Backup and Restore.
2. Select a real `.spbackup` file.
3. Enter password.
4. Click Import Backup directly without clicking Verify.
5. Confirm the backend import runs, success toast appears, and server/group
   lists refresh.
6. Repeat with wrong password and confirm a visible Chinese error appears.

## 2026-06-17: Backup Import Upsert and Verify Removal

Current baseline before this fix:
`a8ec46e2d6c4dae8bc1ae3e34682b90abc734f1a`.

Scope:

- Changed backup import persistence semantics and Settings backup UI only.
- No SSH, Monitor, Terminal, SFTP, workspace, `DisconnectServer`,
  `SecretStore`, Key Vault metadata model, Credential Resolver, or runtime
  architecture changes.

Root cause:

- Repeated import used name-conflict renaming instead of matching servers by
  natural identity.
- `uniqueName()` generated `(...导入 N...)` names and inserted duplicate
  connection rows.
- Duplicate Key Vault private-key paths were skipped without mapping the backup
  key ID to the existing local Key Vault row, so dependent servers could be
  skipped on repeated import.
- The Verify Backup UI path added friction and made users think verification was
  required before import.

Fix:

- Server import now upserts by `host + port + username`.
- Existing matching servers are updated in `connections`; no duplicate server
  is inserted.
- Upsert updates non-sensitive metadata such as name, host, port, username,
  group, auth metadata, private-key path/source, Key Vault ID, refresh interval,
  and optional host fingerprint.
- Existing `credential_refs` rows are untouched, so saved passwords and saved
  passphrase references are preserved.
- Groups are reused by name instead of renamed.
- Key Vault entries are reused by private-key path and mapped to the imported
  backup key ID.
- The old import-name suffix generator is no longer used by backup import.
- Settings -> Backup and Restore no longer shows a Verify Backup button or
  preview panel.
- Import options are visible directly before the Import button.
- Result UI no longer displays a "renamed" count.

Tests:

- Repeated backup import keeps the connection count stable.
- Repeated backup import does not create renamed duplicate server rows.
- Repeated backup import preserves existing credential refs.
- Reused Key Vault paths still allow dependent servers to upsert.
- Settings UI has no Verify Backup button.
- Direct Import Backup still calls the backend and emits success events.

Validation:

- `gofmt`: passed.
- `go vet ./...`: passed.
- `go test ./...`: passed.
- `cmd.exe /c npm run type-check`: passed.
- `cmd.exe /c npm run test -- --run SettingsView`: 11 tests passed.
- `cmd.exe /c npm run test -- --run`: 31 files, 178 tests passed.
- `cmd.exe /c npm run build`: passed.
- `wails build`: passed for Windows amd64.

Windows executable:

- Path: `build/bin/ServerPilot.exe`
- SHA-256: `B99FF7F9C50DE7F59533AE4EF934448F51FAE9FC0D4EA608F891FF5BEAF2A5D0`

Recommended user check:

1. Import a backup once and confirm servers appear.
2. Import the same backup again.
3. Confirm matching servers are updated, not duplicated.
4. Confirm no server name receives an "import" suffix.
5. Confirm saved credentials on an existing matching server still work.
6. Confirm the Settings backup page has no Verify Backup button.

## 2026-06-17: Full Encrypted Backup Mode

Current baseline before this stage:
`4fe222c06ee7cc6bc8483beaca554c6b09984996`.

Stage: full backup mode for saved credentials.

Windows executable: `build/bin/ServerPilot.exe`

Windows executable SHA-256:
`A9F3B25C364089E0B0AFF43F3F5A6258912F7362452CC8C6E4EB19F9A11E3660`

### Scope

- Changed backup export/import logic, Settings backup UI, generated Wails
  backup models, and backup docs only.
- Did not modify SSH, Terminal, Monitor, SFTP runtime architecture,
  `DisconnectServer`, workspace state, CredentialResolver, SecretStore
  platform contract, or Key Vault metadata-only SQLite model.

### Implemented

- Added backup mode `standard | full`.
- Standard backup remains the default and does not read or export saved
  SecretStore values.
- Full backup requires a user backup password and an additional dangerous
  confirmation in Settings -> Backup.
- Full backup exports saved SSH passwords, local private-key passphrases, and
  Key Vault passphrases by reading SecretStore values referenced by SQLite
  metadata.
- Full backup does not export SecretStore keys, credential refs, private-key
  file contents, terminal I/O, logs, SFTP file contents, or workspace state.
- Full import restores mapped SecretStore values to new local references, then
  updates SQLite credential refs.
- If SecretStore writes succeed but ref persistence fails, newly written
  SecretStore entries are deleted.
- Public Wails `ImportBackup` result exposes only counts such as
  `secretsRestored`; internal secret restore values are not part of the
  frontend API model.
- Settings UI shows standard/full export choices, a high-risk warning, and
  full-backup result counts.

### Tests Added Or Updated

- Full export/import restores saved SSH password, saved local private-key
  passphrase, and saved Key Vault passphrase.
- Full backup envelope test confirms plaintext secret values and original
  SecretStore refs are not visible in the `.spbackup` outer JSON.
- Settings backup tests verify default standard mode, full-mode dangerous
  confirmation, and `ExportBackup` receiving `mode: "full"`.

### Validation

- `gofmt -w app.go internal\domain\backup.go internal\backup\service.go internal\backup\service_test.go internal\persistence\backup.go`: passed.
- `go vet ./...`: passed.
- `go test ./...`: passed.

- `cmd.exe /c npm run test -- --run` from `frontend`: 31 files, 179 tests passed.
- `cmd.exe /c npm run build` from `frontend`: passed with existing chunk-size warning.
- `wails build`: passed for Windows amd64.

2. Export a full backup and confirm the high-risk confirmation appears.
3. Import the full backup into a fresh profile and confirm saved password and
   passphrase connections can connect without re-entering credentials.
4. Open the `.spbackup` in a text editor and confirm secret values and
   SecretStore refs are not visible in the outer JSON.
5. Confirm private-key file contents are not copied into the backup.

## 2026-06-17: Isolated Local Terminal Runtime

Current baseline before this stage:
`e6e9b19f521cc5772a6f08c14c76156f13b5c859`.

Stage: Local Terminal MVP.

Windows executable: `build/bin/ServerPilot.exe`

Windows executable SHA-256:
`E5D3C8ED430A0D42D78F8924A594347C339F8B06E9632E54E70212531E62FD62`

### Scope

- Added Local Terminal as an independent runtime.
- Did not modify SSH runtime, Terminal manager/session lifecycle,
  Monitor runtime, SFTP runtime, `DisconnectServer(serverID)`, server
  workspace state, CredentialResolver, SecretStore, Key Vault, or
  backup/import/export behavior.
- Local terminal sessions do not use server IDs and are not server workspaces.

### Implemented

- Added `internal/localterminal` manager with cancellable per-session workers,
  session map, input queue, PTY output reader, idempotent close, and `CloseAll`.
- Added domain models for local terminal open/write/resize/state/output/error.
- Added typed Wails methods:
  - `OpenLocalTerminal`;
  - `WriteLocalTerminal`;
  - `ResizeLocalTerminal`;
  - `CloseLocalTerminal`;
  - `ListLocalTerminals`;
  - `GetLocalTerminalState`.
- Added separate Wails events:
  - `localterminal:output`;
  - `localterminal:state`;
  - `localterminal:error`.
- Added `github.com/aymanbagabas/go-pty` for PTY/ConPTY access.
- Windows shell resolution prefers `pwsh.exe`, then `powershell.exe`, then
  `cmd.exe`.
- Local sessions use `local-<random>` IDs.
- App shutdown closes all local terminal sessions.
- Added frontend local terminal store and `LocalTerminalView.vue`.
- Added topbar and `+` menu entries for opening a local terminal.
- Local terminal appears in workspace tabs visually alongside SSH tabs, but it
  is isolated from server workspace state.
- Closing local terminal tabs calls `CloseLocalTerminal` only.
- Opening or activating SSH server workspaces clears the active local terminal
  view without closing local sessions.

### Security Notes

- Local terminal input, output, environment variables, shell history, cwd
  history, command content, ANSI buffer, and xterm buffer are not written to
  SQLite, logs, localStorage, or backup.
- Local terminal output uses base64 runtime events and is not logged.
- Only sanitized lifecycle errors are surfaced.
- Local terminal does not participate in command history, favorites,
  completion, monitor, SFTP, SSH credential resolution, Key Vault, backup,
  import, or export.

- `go vet ./...`: passed.

- `cmd.exe /c npm run test -- --run` from `frontend`: 34 files, 192 tests passed.
- `cmd.exe /c npm run build` from `frontend`: passed with existing chunk-size warning.
- `wails build`: passed for Windows amd64.

4. Confirm Ctrl+C reaches the local terminal.
5. Resize the window and confirm PTY size follows.

## 2026-06-17: Terminal Command Completion

Current baseline before this stage:
`c283764b9fc3b1044f47f1c77b3eeb8c78b84615`.

Stage: terminal command completion.

Windows executable: `build/bin/ServerPilot.exe`

Windows executable SHA-256:
`0BF445B8E1C1B89789E4FA4EFD189CE6CBD90975E8CADB7040D5C4BA1857173A`

### Scope

- Added completion as an attachment layer only.
- Did not modify SSH runtime, Terminal manager/session lifecycle, Monitor
  runtime, SFTP runtime, `DisconnectServer`, workspace state,
  CredentialResolver, SecretStore, Key Vault, or terminal PTY lifecycle.
- Did not change backup/import/export behavior.

### Implemented

- Added typed `ListCommandSuggestions` API.
- Added `ListCommandSuggestionsRequest` and `CommandSuggestion` domain models.
- Extended `internal/commands.Service` to combine:
  - current-server favorites;
  - current-group favorites;
  - global favorites;
  - current-server command history;
  - static builtin Linux command templates.
- Reused migration 6 tables. No migration 7 was added.
- Added static safe builtin templates such as `ls -la`, `df -h`,
  `systemctl status`, `journalctl -u`, `docker ps`, and `kubectl get pods`.
- Added prefix filtering, source ordering, deduplication by normalized command,
  recency/use-count tie-breakers, and 20-result limit.
- Added frontend Ctrl+Space completion overlay inside `TerminalView`.
- Kept Ctrl+Shift+H and Ctrl+Shift+P behavior unchanged.
- Kept Tab pass-through to remote shell while completion is closed.
- Completion-open keyboard behavior:
  - ArrowUp/ArrowDown selects candidates;
  - Enter inserts without executing;
  - Ctrl+Enter executes;
  - Tab accepts current candidate;
  - Esc closes.
- Added suffix-only insertion when the current clean line is a prefix of the
  candidate.
- Added dirty-buffer and alternate-screen guards to prevent unsafe insertion
  in unknown line state or programs like vim/top.

### Security Notes

- Completion does not record terminal output, stdout/stderr, ANSI buffers,
  unsubmitted input, remote file contents, SFTP content, passwords,
  passphrases, private-key contents, or remote editor buffers.
- Sensitive prefixes and sensitive candidate commands are filtered.
- Filtered original text is not logged and is not shown in toast details.
- Builtin templates are not stored in SQLite.
- Completion data is not included in standard or full backup/export.

## 2026-06-17: Terminal Command History And Favorites

Current baseline before this stage:
`8ae3a597e630661ee59d1bfede2fbcc1cfe9e6ba`.

Stage: terminal command history and grouped command favorites.

Windows executable: `build/bin/ServerPilot.exe`

Windows executable SHA-256:
`004273EC6F2E53DCD8F5545D97C70E4240C270BBB4C3FD75E72439A5708C0ABC`

### Scope

- Added command history/favorites as an attachment layer only.
- Did not modify SSH runtime, Terminal manager/session lifecycle,
  Monitor runtime, SFTP runtime, `DisconnectServer`, workspace state,
  CredentialResolver, SecretStore, or Key Vault.

### Implemented

- Added migration 6 with `command_history` and `command_favorites`.
- Added typed Wails APIs for history list/record/delete/clear and favorites
  list/create/update/delete/use-count.
- Added backend `internal/commands.Service` with safety filtering.
- History records only submitted terminal command lines after Enter.
- Empty commands, control sequences, multi-line paste content, >1000 character
  commands, and common secret-bearing patterns are skipped.
- Consecutive duplicate commands update the latest row instead of duplicating.
- History retention is capped at 2000 rows per server.
- Added grouped favorites with `global`, `group`, and `server` scopes.
- Added command palette overlay opened from terminal context menu, status bar,
  Ctrl+Shift+H, or Ctrl+Shift+P.
- History/favorite actions support insert, explicit execute, favorite, edit,
  and delete. Insert does not send Enter; execute does.
- If there is no active online terminal, command actions show a toast and do
  not create or connect a terminal.
- Command history and favorites are excluded from standard and full backup.

### Security Notes

- Terminal output, stdout/stderr, ANSI buffers, unsubmitted input, remote file
  contents, SFTP data, passwords, passphrases, private-key contents, and
  skipped sensitive commands are not persisted.
- The frontend skips the next submitted line after password/passphrase-looking
  terminal output.
- Suspicious favorite commands require a confirmation dialog before saving.
- Skipped command text is not logged or shown in toast details.

## 2026-06-17: Terminal / Layout / Progress Visibility Regression Fixes

Current baseline before this stage:
`853e1104b5ccc844fc24eb670e0e0092b0476f23`.

Stage: regression fixes after the isolated Local Terminal MVP.

Windows executable: `build/bin/ServerPilot.exe`

Windows executable SHA-256:
`7B79B976919EE86325C469D241E50534F3ACA11F1CEB990BBAD55F2AB8C50D99`

### Scope

- Fixed real-machine regressions only.
- Did not add SCP fallback, recursive transfer, Local Terminal History, macOS
  Keychain, Docker, port forwarding, or any new product feature.
- Did not modify SSH runtime architecture, Terminal backend PTY lifecycle,
  Monitor runtime, SFTP runtime, `DisconnectServer`, workspace state model,
  SecretStore, Key Vault, CredentialResolver, or Backup / Import / Export.

### Implemented

- `TerminalView.vue` now routes custom clipboard paste through
  `terminal.paste(text)`, letting xterm normalize CRLF/LF to terminal carriage
  returns and preserving a single terminal input path.
- Multiline paste remains a passive command-history observation case: the
  OpenSSL multiline paste regression is sent as one `WriteTerminal` call and is
  not inserted into command history or completion.
- Added IME protection in the SSH terminal custom key handler. If
  `event.isComposing`, `key === "Process"`, `code === "Process"`, or
  `keyCode === 229`, shortcut handling returns control to xterm/browser input.
- Added Unicode input regression coverage for `echo 中文测试`.
- Moved terminal padding from `.terminal-view` to `.terminal-view .xterm` so
  FitAddon subtracts the padding when calculating terminal rows.
- Compact monitor split layout now clamps invalid stored heights and treats a
  stored zero height as missing.
- Collapsed compact monitor panes now use `minmax(0, 1fr)` for the visible pane.
- Mount panel CSS is now a fixed header plus `minmax(0, 1fr)` scroll body, which
  prevents double-scroll and clipped mount lists in collapsed monitor mode.
- Added `DEV_PROGRESS.md` for user-visible progress reporting.
- Updated `AI_BRIEF.md`.

### Tests Added Or Updated

- `TerminalView.command-history.test.ts`
  - Multiline CRLF clipboard paste goes through xterm once and calls
    `WriteTerminal` once.
  - Multiline paste does not call command history or completion.
  - IME composing Ctrl+Space passes through and does not open completion.
  - Unicode input reaches `WriteTerminal` and command history records submitted
    Unicode commands.
- `CompactMonitorSidebar.test.ts`
  - Stored split height `0` is treated as missing.
  - Collapsed monitor and collapsed mount panes use the expected full-height
    classes and grid rows.
- `style.tokens.test.ts`
  - Terminal padding stays on `.xterm`.
  - Mount panel remains a fixed header plus one scrolling body.

  close event and the parent calls `store.closeSession(sessionID)`, not
  `DisconnectServer`.

  - Ctrl/Cmd+A on page chrome is prevented;
  - inputs, textarea, select, contenteditable, CodeMirror, and xterm areas are
    allowed through.
- Added non-sensitive terminal preferences:
  - `terminalCopyOnSelectEnabled`;
  - `terminalRightClickPasteEnabled`.

- Old settings and old backup settings default the new terminal clipboard
  preferences to enabled.
- Settings page now exposes SSH terminal selected-copy and right-click paste
  toggles without re-enabling Local Terminal.
- Terminal selected-copy uses xterm selection APIs and writes only to the system
  clipboard.

  xterm `onData -> WriteTerminal` input path.

  actions remain reachable.

  bottom-right.
- Changed `CommandPalette` from a centered modal to a right-side lightweight
  overlay below modal/toast z-index.
- `commands` Pinia store now normalizes null/undefined history, favorites, and
  suggestions API responses to `[]`.
- Favorite save/use paths no longer call `findIndex` on possibly null local
  state.
- Favorite rows defensively normalize tags, sort order, description, and use
  count.
- Backend command history/favorite list paths now return non-nil empty slices so
  Wails JSON returns `[]`, not `null`.
- Added SFTP toolbar `向上` button before Home. It reuses existing parent
  navigation and is disabled at remote root.
- SFTP `显示隐藏文件` toolbar and menu buttons now show a blue active state.

### Root Causes

1. Duplicate top server tabs: `WorkspaceTabs.vue` rendered every terminal
   session as a top tab whenever a server had sessions, so same-server new
   terminals created multiple same-name top tabs.
2. Misleading close behavior: server top tab close/menu entries were using
   server-wide disconnect semantics while showing workspace/session-like copy.
3. Ctrl+A whole-app selection: WebView2 kept browser default select-all behavior
   on non-input app chrome.
4. Command panel disappearing / cannot reopen: command favorites state could be
   `null` when backend or local state returned/polluted null, and `findIndex`
   then threw an uncaught TypeError.
5. Favorite save failure: save/update/use paths assumed local favorites was
   always an array and that tags/sortOrder were always normalized.
6. History/favorites null responses: Go nil slices encode as JSON `null`; list
   APIs now return non-nil empty slices.

### Tests Added Or Updated

- Backend:
  - command history/favorite empty lists return non-nil empty slices;
  - global favorite scope does not require server/group ID;
  - server/group favorite scopes validate required IDs;
  - nil/empty tags and default sort order are safe;
  - legacy terminal clipboard settings normalize on.
- Frontend:
  - same-server terminals render one top workspace tab;
  - server tab menu wording is server-wide and emits disconnect intent;
  - command store normalizes null list responses;
  - command store survives null local favorites during save/use;
  - command panel stays open when switching History/Favorites;
  - null backend lists render empty command lists;
  - terminal selected-copy and right-click paste settings work;
  - right-click paste uses xterm paste/onData path;
  - disabled right-click paste keeps context menu;
  - Settings exposes and saves terminal clipboard toggles while Local Terminal
    stays hidden;
  - SFTP `向上` order/root disabled behavior;
  - SFTP hidden-file active state;
  - Ctrl/Cmd+A guard allows only expected editable/terminal targets;
  - command button is inside terminal pane, not status bar.

### Validation

- `gofmt -w (git ls-files '*.go')`: passed.
- `go vet ./...`: passed.
- `go test ./...`: passed.
- `cmd.exe /c npm run type-check`: passed before and after Wails binding generation.
- `cmd.exe /c npm run test -- --run`: passed, 39 files and 247 tests.
- `cmd.exe /c npm run build`: passed with the existing Vite chunk-size warning.
- `wails build`: passed for Windows amd64.

Note: an initial `wails build` was started in parallel with `npm run build` and
failed because Vite was cleaning/replacing `frontend/dist` while Wails tried to
embed hashed assets. Re-running `wails build` serially after the frontend build
completed passed. This was a command sequencing issue, not a code/build defect.

### User Should Test

1. Create multiple SSH terminals on the same server; confirm there is only one
   top server tab.
2. Right-click the server tab; confirm `关闭服务器工作区` and misleading
   `仅关闭当前终端` are gone.
3. Use terminal context menu `仅关闭当前终端`; confirm only that terminal closes.
4. Close/disconnect server tab; confirm all terminal/SFTP/monitor/reconnect work
   for that server stops through normal server disconnect.
5. Press Ctrl+A on empty app chrome; confirm the whole UI is not selected.
6. Press Ctrl+A in input, remote editor, and SSH terminal; confirm each behaves
   correctly.
7. Select terminal text with selected-copy enabled; confirm clipboard updates.
8. Disable selected-copy and confirm selecting terminal text no longer copies.
9. Right-click terminal with no selection and right-click paste enabled; confirm
   clipboard text pastes.
10. Disable right-click paste and confirm right-click opens menu instead.
11. Confirm top-right button reads `菜单`.
12. Confirm `命令` button is in the SSH terminal pane bottom-right.
13. Open Command panel, switch History/Favorites repeatedly, close and reopen.
14. Create, edit, delete global/server/group command favorites.
15. Restart app and confirm command history still loads from SQLite.
16. In SFTP, confirm `向上` is before Home and disabled at `/`.
17. Toggle hidden files and confirm button turns blue when active.
18. Confirm SSH terminal, SFTP transfer/delete/SCP fallback, Monitor, Backup,
    and Local Terminal quarantine are not regressed.

### Still Not Done

- Pause/resume transfer.
- Shell-based SCP remote listing.
- Stable Windows Local Terminal rewrite/proof.
- Docker / port forwarding.
- macOS Keychain native implementation.

## 2026-06-18: Stable Windows Local Terminal Proof / Quarantine

Baseline commit:
`f96315d301644913445de41f905e9aefdb1c47de`

Stage:
Revisit Windows Local Terminal after previous real-machine failures. The task
allowed two outcomes: enable only after real GUI smoke passes, or keep Local
Terminal quarantined. This round chose the safe quarantine outcome.

Windows executable:
`build/bin/ServerPilot.exe`

Windows executable SHA-256:
`4D942A03F32B7E30C956932D204C1EC64A263D629A1E47431C69D8A0A8512DA3`

### Scope

- Did not modify SSH runtime architecture.
- Did not modify SSH Terminal PTY lifecycle.
- Did not modify Monitor runtime.
- Did not modify SFTP/SCP runtime.
- Did not modify `DisconnectServer(serverID)`.
- Did not modify workspace state model.
- Did not modify SecretStore or Key Vault.
- Did not modify Backup / Import / Export.
- Did not modify Command History / Favorites / Completion data model.

### Decision

Local Terminal remains default-disabled and unavailable to users.

The existing Windows path is still the old PTY-wrapper based implementation
with Windows process flags. It is not the required direct ConPTY adapter using
`CreatePseudoConsole`, `ResizePseudoConsole`, `ClosePseudoConsole`,
`STARTUPINFOEX`, pseudo-console process attributes, and owned pipe lifecycle.

Because the enabled PowerShell/cmd GUI smoke was not run and cannot be claimed
as passing, the UI entry must not be exposed.

### Implemented

- Added a hard release gate inside `internal/localterminal`.
- The existing `SERVERPILOT_LOCAL_TERMINAL_EXPERIMENTAL=1` environment flag no
  longer enables the unstable runtime by itself.
- `OpenLocalTerminal` continues to return `LOCAL_TERMINAL_DISABLED`.
- Disabled `WriteLocalTerminal`, `ResizeLocalTerminal`, and
  `GetLocalTerminalState` return disabled before entering runtime.
- Disabled `CloseLocalTerminal` remains a safe no-op for stale local tab IDs.
- Disabled `ListLocalTerminals` returns an empty slice rather than nil.
- Settings Local Terminal capability loading now handles empty/null shell
  options defensively.
- `+` menu and Settings Local Terminal shell preference stay hidden while
  capabilities are disabled.

### Tests Added / Updated

- Local terminal capability test now verifies that the env flag alone cannot
  enable the quarantined feature.
- App-level disabled test now installs a panic PTY factory and proves disabled
  Open/Write/Resize/GetState do not enter runtime or start a PTY.
- App-level disabled test verifies `ListLocalTerminals` returns an empty slice.
- Existing frontend tests continue to verify:
  - `+` menu hides Local Terminal while disabled;
  - Settings hides Local Terminal shell preference while disabled;
  - stale local tabs can be removed safely;
  - local terminal close does not call `DisconnectServer`.

### Windows GUI Smoke

Default-disabled smoke was executed:

1. Built `build/bin/ServerPilot.exe`.
2. Started the EXE.
3. Waited until a real main window handle existed.
4. Sent `WM_CLOSE` to the main window.
5. Confirmed normal process exit.
6. Confirmed no child processes before close.
7. Confirmed no child processes after close.
8. Confirmed no new residual `ServerPilot.exe` process after close.

Enabled Local Terminal smoke was not executed. PowerShell and cmd were not
tested because the runtime remains gated until a direct ConPTY adapter exists.

### Validation

- `gofmt -w app.go app_test.go internal/localterminal/manager.go internal/localterminal/manager_test.go`: passed.
- `go vet ./...`: passed.
- `go test ./...`: passed.
- `cmd.exe /c npm run type-check` from `frontend`: passed.
- `cmd.exe /c npm run test -- --run` from `frontend`: passed, 39 files and 274 tests.
- `cmd.exe /c npm run build` from `frontend`: passed with existing Vite chunk-size warning.
- `wails build`: passed for Windows amd64.
- `git diff --check`: passed.

### Next Local Terminal Work

To enable Local Terminal later, implement a direct Windows ConPTY adapter first:

- CreatePseudoConsole / ResizePseudoConsole / ClosePseudoConsole.
- STARTUPINFOEX with PROC_THREAD_ATTRIBUTE_PSEUDOCONSOLE.
- CreateProcessW with EXTENDED_STARTUPINFO_PRESENT.
- CREATE_NO_WINDOW and CREATE_NEW_PROCESS_GROUP.
- Owned stdin/stdout pipes and session-scoped cancellation.
- No GenerateConsoleCtrlEvent broadcast.
- Real GUI smoke for PowerShell and cmd before exposing UI.

## 2026-06-18: Direct ConPTY Flag Conflict Decision

Baseline commit:
`02c2a89083ddfedf3372704706c3cf6426cc6ff7`

Stage:
Resolve the Windows Direct ConPTY `CREATE_NO_WINDOW` conflict and decide
whether Local Terminal can be enabled.

Windows executable:
`build/bin/ServerPilot.exe`

Windows executable SHA-256:
`403939C30DF232DF564139160E4CA08B0CEF937F11624FB5FAE849CC65DAEE8C`

### Scope

- Did not modify SSH runtime architecture.
- Did not modify SSH Terminal PTY lifecycle.
- Did not modify Monitor runtime.
- Did not modify SFTP/SCP runtime.
- Did not modify `DisconnectServer(serverID)`.
- Did not modify workspace state model.
- Did not modify SecretStore or Key Vault.
- Did not modify Backup / Import / Export.
- Did not modify Command History / Favorites / Completion data model.

### Product Decision

Local Terminal remains default-disabled and unavailable to users.

The release requirement is no longer a literal `CREATE_NO_WINDOW` flag. The
actual release requirement is verified GUI behavior: no external PowerShell/cmd
window, embedded prompt/output, working input, `echo
serverpilot-local-terminal-smoke`, shell `exit` does not close ServerPilot,
local terminal tab close does not close ServerPilot, and no residual child
shell process.

The current Direct ConPTY adapter uses:

- `EXTENDED_STARTUPINFO_PRESENT`;
- `CREATE_NEW_PROCESS_GROUP`;
- no `CREATE_NO_WINDOW`;
- no `GenerateConsoleCtrlEvent`.

`CREATE_NO_WINDOW` remains excluded because backend comparison showed it breaks
ConPTY input/output on this Windows runtime.

### Important Evidence Rule

An observed local terminal open/close sequence with an approximately 4-second
interval must not be counted as Codex GUI smoke evidence. The user clarified
that this was likely their manual operation.

Enabled GUI smoke was attempted but not completed with reliable evidence for
prompt visibility, echo output, input, shell `exit`, tab close, or external
window behavior. Therefore the release gate remains closed.

### Implemented / Kept

- Reverted the temporary release gate used during attempted enabled GUI smoke.
- Confirmed `SERVERPILOT_LOCAL_TERMINAL_EXPERIMENTAL=1` still does not enable
  Local Terminal while the release gate is closed.
- Kept the Direct ConPTY adapter implementation.
- Added backend Direct ConPTY PowerShell smoke coverage.
- Kept backend Direct ConPTY cmd smoke coverage.
- `OpenLocalTerminal` remains disabled in product builds and does not start
  shell/PTY while disabled.
- `+` menu and Settings Local Terminal shell preference remain hidden.

### Windows GUI Smoke

Default-disabled smoke was executed:

1. Built `build/bin/ServerPilot.exe`.
2. Started the EXE.
3. Waited until a real main window handle existed.
4. Closed the window through the main window close path.
5. Confirmed process exit.
6. Confirmed child processes after close: 0.
7. Confirmed shell child processes after close: 0.
8. Confirmed residual `ServerPilot.exe` processes after close: 0.

Enabled Local Terminal GUI smoke was not proven:

- cmd GUI smoke: not proven.
- PowerShell GUI smoke: not proven.
- no external shell window: not proven.
- shell `exit` preserving ServerPilot: not proven.
- local terminal tab close preserving ServerPilot: not proven.

### Validation

- `gofmt -w internal/localterminal/process_windows_test.go`: passed.
- `go vet ./...`: passed.
- `go test ./...`: passed.
- `cmd.exe /c npm run type-check` from `frontend`: passed.
- `cmd.exe /c npm run test -- --run` from `frontend`: passed, 39 files and
  274 tests.
- `cmd.exe /c npm run build` from `frontend`: passed with existing Vite
  chunk-size warning.
- `wails build`: passed for Windows amd64.
- `git diff --check`: passed.

### Next Local Terminal Work

The next attempt should automate or otherwise make reliable the enabled GUI
smoke for both cmd and PowerShell. Only after that smoke proves no external
window, real input/output, safe shell `exit`, safe tab close, and process
cleanup should the release gate be opened.

## 2026-06-18: Restore Windows Local Terminal Entry

Baseline commit:
`8e0eb556f174d92a83a5efb082a427d1253c1e79`

Restore Windows Local Terminal entry after user true-machine ConPTY validation.

Windows executable:

`DA448F662F390B3FA2407577CCF2CB8C0F98F04B113F662EC0E8BB5355E4686F`

### Decision

Local Terminal is restored on Windows when Direct ConPTY is available.

The previous automated screenshot smoke instability is not a valid reason to
keep hiding the feature after the user manually confirmed cmd and PowerShell
can open in the real Windows GUI.

### User-Confirmed Evidence

The user manually tested the built app and confirmed:

- cmd opens.
- PowerShell opens.

### Root Cause

`ExperimentalEnabled()` still returned false unless a release/smoke gate was
opened. Since Codex could not reliably prove enabled GUI behavior through
screenshots, the previous round kept the gate closed and hid the entry. That
was too conservative for the current product stage because user true-machine
testing had already proven cmd/PowerShell launch capability.

### Implemented

- Removed the smoke/release gate from Local Terminal capabilities.
- Added runtime availability detection:
  - platform must be Windows;
  - Direct ConPTY APIs must exist;
  - shell resolver must find cmd or PowerShell.
- `GetLocalTerminalCapabilities()` now returns enabled/supported on Windows
  when those runtime checks pass.
- `OpenLocalTerminal()` is no longer blocked by screenshot smoke status.
- `+` menu shows `本地终端` when capabilities are supported.
- Settings shows Local Terminal shell preference when supported.
- Settings shows an unsupported Local Terminal card instead of silently hiding
  it when unavailable.
- Opening failure still returns an error and does not create a blank local tab.

### Architecture / Safety

- Did not modify SSH runtime architecture.
- Did not modify SSH Terminal PTY lifecycle.
- Did not modify Monitor runtime.
- Did not modify SFTP/SCP runtime.
- Did not modify `DisconnectServer(serverID)`.
- Did not modify workspace state model.
- Did not modify SecretStore or Key Vault.
- Did not modify Backup / Import / Export.
- Local Terminal does not use `serverID`.
- Local Terminal does not participate in `DisconnectServer(serverID)`.
- Local Terminal input/output is not logged.

### Tests Added / Updated

- Backend capabilities now expect Windows Direct ConPTY availability to enable
  Local Terminal.
- Runtime availability tests cover ConPTY presence, shell resolver success,
  shell resolver failure, and non-Windows disablement.
- App tests cover capability propagation, open failure without leftover
  sessions, and independent local session close.
- Frontend tests cover `+` menu Local Terminal visibility when supported.
- Frontend store test covers OpenLocalTerminal failure not creating a blank tab.
- Settings tests cover supported shell preference and unsupported disabled card.

### Validation

- `gofmt`: passed.
- `go test ./internal/localterminal -count=1 -v -timeout=90s`: passed.
- Targeted app Local Terminal tests: passed.
- `cmd.exe /c npm run type-check` from `frontend`: passed.
- `cmd.exe /c npm run test -- --run` from `frontend`: passed, 39 files and
  276 tests.
- `go vet ./...`: passed.
- `go test ./...`: passed.
- `cmd.exe /c npm run build` from `frontend`: passed with existing Vite
  chunk-size warning.
- `wails build`: passed for Windows amd64.
- `git diff --check`: passed.

### Manual Test Checklist

1. Start ServerPilot.
2. Open the `+` menu and confirm `本地终端` is visible.
3. Open Settings and confirm Local Terminal shell preference is visible.
4. Select cmd and open Local Terminal.
5. Confirm cmd has output and accepts input.

That remains a test-automation gap, not a reason to hide the Windows Local

## 2026-06-18: Local Terminal Productization

Baseline commit:
`b07a3f280abd6f099f8525a59b9bc1f2e008ad24`

Stage:
Local Terminal productization after user true-machine validation.

Windows executable:
`build/bin/ServerPilot.exe`

Windows executable SHA-256:
`97AC02822C0BC0ED1E554F0DB4ADAFB91218559A0ADC6AD62A5BDD43175E404C`

### Result

CMD and PowerShell can now be used as formal Windows Local Terminal entries
from the `+` menu. The old Settings-level "本地终端默认 Shell" selector is no
longer shown.

### User-Confirmed Input

Before this round, the user had already tested the real Windows GUI build and
confirmed:

- cmd opens and accepts input.
- PowerShell opens and accepts input.
- `echo serverpilot-local-terminal-smoke` works.
- shell `exit` does not close ServerPilot.
- local terminal tab close does not close ServerPilot.
- SSH / SFTP / SCP / Monitor remain normal.

### Implemented

- Removed Local Terminal default shell selector from Settings UI.
- Kept `localTerminalShellPreference` only for settings compatibility.
- `+` menu now exposes:
  - 添加服务器
  - 添加分组
  - CMD
  - PowerShell
  - CMD（管理员）
  - PowerShell（管理员）
- `OpenLocalTerminal` now requires typed menu intent:
  - `shellKind: cmd | powershell`
  - `elevated: boolean`
- Local Terminal API response/state now includes:
  - `shellKind`
  - `shellName`
  - `elevated`
  - `title`
  - `status`
- Capabilities now include:
  - `conptyAvailable`
  - `isProcessElevated`
  - `supportsElevation`
  - `shellOptions`
  - `adminShellOptions`
- CMD/PowerShell local tabs participate in the same `WorkspaceTabs` ordering as
  SSH terminal and workspace tabs.
- Local tabs are not pinned to a separate right-side group.
- Later-created SSH tabs can appear to the right of existing CMD/PowerShell
  tabs.
- LocalTerminalView now shares the SSH terminal clipboard composable:
  `useTerminalClipboard`.
- Local Terminal honors:
  - terminal copy-on-select setting;
  - terminal right-click paste setting.
- Right-click paste writes through `WriteLocalTerminal`.
- Shell `exited` / `failed` state auto-removes only the matching local tab.
- Late local terminal events cannot resurrect a closed/auto-removed local tab.

### Admin Local Terminal Strategy

Implemented minimum safe behavior, not full relaunch:

- If ServerPilot is already elevated:
  - CMD（管理员） and PowerShell（管理员） are allowed;
  - they open through Direct ConPTY;
  - no external cmd/PowerShell window is spawned by the app layer.
- If ServerPilot is not elevated:
  - the frontend shows a Chinese in-app explanation;
  - no fake tab is created;
  - no external shell window is spawned;
  - backend still protects elevated requests with `NEED_ELEVATED_RELAUNCH`.

Full `ShellExecuteEx / runas` relaunch with auto-open admin terminal is still
future work.

### Architecture / Safety

- Did not modify SSH runtime architecture.
- Did not modify SSH Terminal PTY lifecycle.
- Did not modify Monitor runtime.
- Did not modify SFTP/SCP runtime.
- Did not modify `DisconnectServer(serverID)`.
- Did not modify workspace state model.
- Did not modify SecretStore or Key Vault.
- Did not modify Backup / Import / Export.
- Did not modify Command History / Favorites / Completion data model.
- Local Terminal does not use `serverID`.
- Local Terminal does not participate in `DisconnectServer(serverID)`.
- Local Terminal does not enter Backup / Export.
- No terminal input/output or clipboard content is logged.

### Validation

- `gofmt`: passed.
- `go vet ./...`: passed.
- `go test ./...`: passed.
- `cmd.exe /c npm run type-check` from `frontend`: passed.
- `cmd.exe /c npm run test -- --run` from `frontend`: passed, 39 files and
  279 tests.
- `cmd.exe /c npm run build` from `frontend`: passed with existing Vite
  chunk-size warning.
- `wails build`: passed for Windows amd64.
- `git diff --check`: passed.

### Important Files

- `internal/domain/localterminal.go`
- `internal/localterminal/manager.go`
- `internal/localterminal/process_windows.go`
- `app.go`
- `frontend/src/App.vue`
- `frontend/src/components/ServerPicker.vue`
- `frontend/src/components/SettingsView.vue`
- `frontend/src/components/WorkspaceTabs.vue`

   PowerShell（管理员） are visible.

## 2026-06-18 - Terminal Paste Selection Fix + SSH Tunnel MVP

### Current Status

- Implemented terminal paste selection cleanup for SSH Terminal and Local
  Terminal CMD/PowerShell.
- Implemented SSH tunnel / port forwarding MVP with local forward, remote
  forward, and dynamic SOCKS5 CONNECT.
- Built Windows EXE:
  `build/bin/ServerPilot.exe`
- SHA-256:
  `E5B5C7A72803C8182708ED9B708438BD25C3E96D5AF6A438AA82230E9A1E54B4`

### Key Changes

- `frontend/src/composables/useTerminalClipboard.ts`
  - suppresses copy-on-select during paste;
  - clears selection and focuses before/after paste.
- `frontend/src/components/TerminalView.vue`
  - right-click paste uses shared cleanup behavior.
- `frontend/src/components/LocalTerminalView.vue`
  - CMD/PowerShell local terminal uses the same cleanup behavior.
- `internal/tunnelmanager`
  - independent tunnel runtime;
  - owns tunnel worker lifecycle, listener/connection cleanup, SOCKS5 parsing,
    and state/error/traffic events.
- `internal/domain/tunnel.go`
  - typed tunnel requests, runtime states, events, and backup profile struct.
- `internal/persistence/tunnels.go`
  - tunnel profile CRUD.
- `internal/persistence/store.go`
  - migration version 9 creates `tunnel_profiles`.
- `internal/persistence/backup.go`
  - backup/export/import includes tunnel profile metadata only.
- `app.go`
  - typed Wails APIs for tunnel profile/runtime control;
  - tunnel events emitted as `tunnel:state`, `tunnel:error`, `tunnel:traffic`.
- `internal/serverlifecycle/coordinator.go`
  - `DisconnectServer(serverID)` now stops that server's tunnels through the
    existing coordinator path.
- `frontend/src/stores/tunnels.ts`
  - tunnel profile/runtime store and event subscription.
- `frontend/src/components/TunnelManagerDialog.vue`
  - modal UI for local/remote/dynamic tunnel profiles and runtime control.
- `frontend/src/components/WorkspaceTabs.vue`
  - top navigation includes `端口转发`.
- `frontend/src/components/TerminalWorkspace.vue`
  - status bar includes `隧道 N` entry.

### Architecture Notes

- Tunnel runtime is separate from SSH Terminal, Monitor, SFTP/SCP, and Local
  Terminal runtimes.
- Tunnel uses its own SSH client; it does not reuse Terminal, Monitor, or SFTP
  sessions.
- StopTunnel only stops the target tunnel.
- Server-wide tunnel shutdown is attached to the existing
  `DisconnectServer(serverID)` coordinator path; external semantics did not
  change.
- Profile persistence stores only non-sensitive metadata.
- Backup includes tunnel profile metadata only; runtime sessions, traffic
  counters, active connections, payloads, and secrets are excluded.
- No tunnel traffic content, clipboard content, terminal input/output,
  passwords, passphrases, private keys, or SecretStore values are logged.

## 2026-06-18 - Docker Container Management MVP

### Summary

Implemented the Docker / container management MVP requested for ServerPilot.
The feature is added as an independent SSH exec subsystem and exposed through a
floating `容器管理` dialog from the top navigation menu. It does not switch the
main workspace and does not rebuild or reuse existing Terminal, SFTP/SCP,
Monitor, Tunnel, or Local Terminal runtime sessions.

### New Backend Runtime

- Added `internal/dockermanager`.
- Docker manager uses independent SSH exec connections per operation/watcher.
- It executes remote Docker CLI commands with JSON/formatted output:
  - `docker version --format '{{json .}}'`
  - `docker ps -a --format '{{json .}}'`
  - `docker stats --no-stream --format '{{json .}}' <container>`
  - `docker inspect <container>`
  - `docker logs --tail <N> <container>`
  - `docker logs -f --tail <N> <container>`
- Go parses Docker CLI output into typed domain structs.
- Frontend does not parse raw Docker CLI text.
- Container IDs are validated with a strict allow-list pattern before command
  construction.
- Container IDs are shell-quoted after validation.
- Docker manager maps common errors to Chinese user-facing messages:
  - Docker not installed.
  - Docker permission denied.
  - Docker daemon not running.
  - Container missing/deleted.

### New Wails API / Events

Added typed APIs:

- `DockerCheck(serverID)`
- `DockerListContainers(request)`
- `DockerStartContainer(request)`
- `DockerStopContainer(request)`
- `DockerRestartContainer(request)`
- `DockerRemoveContainer(request)`
- `DockerGetContainerLogs(request)`
- `DockerStartLogStream(request)`
- `DockerStopLogStream(request)`
- `DockerGetContainerInspectSummary(request)`
- `DockerGetContainerStats(request)`
- `DockerStartStatsWatch(request)`
- `DockerStopStatsWatch(request)`

Added events:

- `docker:state`
- `docker:containers`
- `docker:logs`
- `docker:stats`
- `docker:error`

Events carry serverID and container/stream/watch IDs where applicable.

### Lifecycle Integration

- Extended `serverlifecycle.Coordinator` with a Docker resource hook.
- `DisconnectServer(serverID)` now stops that server's Docker log streams and
  stats watchers through the existing coordinator path.
- Stop log stream / stop stats watcher does not call `DisconnectServer`.
- `App.shutdown` calls Docker manager `StopAll`.
- Frontend `disconnectServer` clears Docker runtime UI state for that server.
- Late log/stats events are filtered by streamID/watchID and server generation.

### Frontend

- Added `frontend/src/stores/docker.ts`.
- Store keeps Docker state isolated by serverID.
- Logs are UI-memory-only and capped at 5000 lines.
- Added `frontend/src/components/DockerManagerDialog.vue`.
- Top navigation menu now includes `容器管理`.
- Dialog supports:
  - server selector;
  - Docker availability status;
  - refresh;
  - search;
  - running/stopped filter;
  - container list;
  - start;
  - stop with confirmation;
  - restart with confirmation;
  - delete stopped container with confirmation;
  - running container delete blocked;
  - recent logs;
  - log follow start/stop;
  - clear current log display;
  - one-shot stats;
  - stats watcher start/stop;
  - inspect summary.

### Security / Persistence

- Container logs are not written to app logs, SQLite, or backup/export.
- Docker inspect full output and Env are not exposed.
- Stats and inspect summaries are not persisted.
- Docker runtime availability/state/list/logs/stats are not included in
  backup/export/import.
- No Docker registry credentials are handled in this MVP.
- No SSH credentials, passwords, passphrases, private keys, SecretStore values,
  terminal IO, file contents, container log contents, or full inspect output
  are logged.
- SecretStore, Key Vault, and Backup secret behavior were not modified.

### Tests Added / Updated

- Backend:
  - Docker check success/not-installed/permission cases.
  - Docker ps JSON parsing.
  - start/stop/restart command construction.
  - remove only stopped containers.
  - logs return content without logging it.
  - log stream events and stop path.
  - stats JSON/unit parsing.
  - inspect summary does not expose Env or mount details.
  - StopServer clears watchers only for target server.
  - container ID injection rejected.
  - backup payload has no Docker runtime fields.
- Lifecycle:
  - `DisconnectServer` stops Docker resource for target server only.
- Frontend:
  - top menu shows `容器管理`.
  - Docker store typed calls/events/log cap/watcher cleanup.
  - Docker dialog open/list/unavailable/search/filter/actions/logs/stats/inspect.
  - dangerous-operation confirmations.
  - running container delete blocked.
  - dialog close stops log/stats watchers.

- `go vet ./...`: passed.
- `go test ./...`: passed.
- `cmd.exe /c npm run type-check` from `frontend`: passed.
- `cmd.exe /c npm run test -- --run` from `frontend`: passed, 43 files and
  322 tests. Existing jsdom canvas warning remains non-fatal.
- `cmd.exe /c npm run build` from `frontend`: passed with existing Vite
  chunk-size warning.
- `wails build`: passed for Windows amd64.

- SHA-256:
  `486053972EB7C3381898D1229DC64FD7291157E31087D7D096BD74E25BDDDA1A`

## 2026-06-19 - Local Terminal Admin Preference And Docker List Layout Fix

### Current Result

- `+` menu no longer shows `CMD（管理员）` or `PowerShell（管理员）`.
- `+` menu still shows normal `CMD` and `PowerShell`.
- Settings now includes `以管理员模式打开本地终端`.
- Opening CMD/PowerShell now derives `elevated` from
  `settings.localTerminalElevatedEnabled`.
- Docker container cards now use a stable table-like layout and no longer rely
  on the previous status/stats shared-column compression.

### Local Terminal Details

- Added `localTerminalElevatedEnabled` to `domain.AppSettings`.
- `CurrentSettingsVersion` is now 6.
- SQLite migration version is now 10 and adds:
  `local_terminal_elevated_enabled INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0`.
- `ServerPicker` emits only shell kind for local terminal menu clicks.
- `App.vue` maps:
  - CMD -> `OpenLocalTerminal({ shellKind: "cmd", elevated: settings.localTerminalElevatedEnabled })`
  - PowerShell -> `OpenLocalTerminal({ shellKind: "powershell", elevated: settings.localTerminalElevatedEnabled })`
- Non-elevated admin mode now asks for confirmation and calls the elevated
  relaunch API instead of creating a fake tab.
- Elevated relaunch uses Windows `ShellExecuteW runas`.
- The elevated process consumes `--serverpilot-open-local-terminal=<shell>`
  once and auto-opens the selected CMD/PowerShell tab.

### Docker Layout Details

- Container rows now have five fixed logical columns:
  checkbox, main info, status, resources, actions.
- Main info uses dedicated classes:
  `.container-name`, `.container-image`, `.container-id`.
- Long names/images/IDs use ellipsis instead of expanding into neighboring
  columns.
- CPU/MEM labels remain in `.container-stats`.
- Action buttons remain in a fixed-width action column.
- Narrow windows use a two-row compact grid with explicit grid areas.
- Batch selection and Stats refresh keep the same row structure.

### Architecture / Security Boundaries

- No change to SSH runtime.
- No change to Terminal PTY lifecycle.
- No change to Monitor runtime.
- No change to SFTP/SCP runtime.
- No change to Tunnel runtime.
- No change to `DisconnectServer(serverID)`.
- No change to workspace state model.
- No change to SecretStore or Key Vault.
- No change to Backup / Import / Export secret behavior.
- Local Terminal remains independent and does not use `serverID`.
- Docker manager remains independent SSH exec; no Docker backend command
  semantics were changed.
- No secrets, terminal IO, Docker logs, private keys, passwords, or passphrases
  were logged or persisted.

### Tests Added / Updated

- Frontend:
  - ServerPicker no longer renders admin CMD/PowerShell entries.
  - ServerPicker emits only `cmd` / `powershell`.
  - Settings renders and saves `以管理员模式打开本地终端`.
  - App passes `elevated=false` when the setting is off.
  - App passes `elevated=true` when the setting is on and the process is
    already elevated.
  - App shows the relaunch confirmation and calls
    `RelaunchElevatedLocalTerminal` when admin mode is requested from a
    non-elevated process.
  - App consumes startup local-terminal requests after elevated relaunch and
    opens the requested shell.
  - Docker rows preserve main/status/stats/actions regions across multi-select
    and Stats refresh.
- Backend:
  - Startup local-terminal request parsing accepts only valid shell kinds and
    is consumed once.
  - Settings service persists `LocalTerminalElevatedEnabled`.
  - SQLite settings read/update round trip includes
    `LocalTerminalElevatedEnabled`.
  - Old database migration version updated to 10 and defaults admin mode off.
