  ellipsis-safe columns.

  - two-row card layout at `760px`;

  - running row stop/restart buttons;

    action overflow, and responsive breakpoints.

## 2026-06-19 - Terminal Profiles & Preferences MVP

### Baseline

- Previous HEAD before this round:
  `7052a38ab4466b126a0128c4564a7996f1d06b67`.
- Task scope: Terminal Profiles & Preferences MVP.
- New EXE: `build/bin/ServerPilot.exe`.
- New EXE SHA-256:
  `30FAE9A6FF7078677505555AD7D7F17C18DF4B8538412B21C4D07A27E203EC1E`.

### Completed

- Added non-sensitive terminal profile model:
  - name;
  - font family;
  - font size;
  - line height;
  - letter spacing;
  - cursor style/blink;
  - scrollback;
  - built-in/custom colors.
- Added migration 11 and settings version 7.
- Added profile CRUD persistence and Wails APIs.
- Added global default profile support.
- Added per-server profile override via `connections.terminal_profile_id`.
- Added Settings -> Terminal profile management UI.
- Added server edit form terminal profile selector.
- SSH Terminal now receives the resolved profile for the active tab server.
- Local Terminal receives the global default profile.
- `Apply to open terminals` bumps a frontend revision that updates xterm
  options and schedules fit. It does not disconnect, recreate xterm, recreate
  PTY, clear buffer, or drop unsubmitted input.
- Backup/export/import now includes:
  - `terminalProfiles`;
  - `defaultTerminalProfileId`;
  - per-server `terminalProfileId`.
- Import remaps custom profile IDs and keeps backup `default` mapped to the
  local built-in default profile.
- ROADMAP marks configurable terminal profiles and preferences complete.

### Safety / Isolation

- Terminal profiles are UI preferences only and are not sensitive.
- No terminal input, output, buffer, clipboard, password, passphrase, private
  key content, or command content is logged or exported by this feature.
- Terminal profiles do not use SecretStore.
- The following were not changed:
  - SSH runtime;
  - Terminal PTY lifecycle;
  - Monitor runtime;
  - SFTP/SCP runtime;
  - Tunnel runtime;
  - Docker runtime;
  - Local Terminal runtime;
  - `DisconnectServer(serverID)`;
  - workspace state model;
  - SecretStore;
  - Key Vault;
  - Backup secret behavior.

### Important Behavior Notes

- Updating a terminal profile in Settings does not automatically reconnect or
  rebuild any terminal session.
- Applying a profile to open terminals updates xterm options in-place and then
  fits the terminal; remote PTY resize only follows normal fit rows/cols
  changes.
- Existing SSH terminal buffer and unsubmitted input are preserved.
- Local Terminal profile application does not restart the shell process.
- Server profile override is used for newly opened SSH terminals for that
  server. Open terminals can be updated with the explicit apply action.

## 2026-06-19 - Terminal Profile Apply And Settings Action UX Fix

### Baseline

- Previous commit: `355d09ed69e8d8cc1ac9a99a0576af8d73ad9b6d`
  (`feat: add terminal profiles and preferences`).
- Task scope: fix Terminal Profiles & Settings user acceptance issues only.

### What Changed

- Added `frontend/src/utils/terminalInstanceRegistry.ts`.
- SSH `TerminalView` and `LocalTerminalView` now register mounted xterm
  instances and unregister on unmount.
- Registry metadata tracks:
  - session ID;
  - terminal kind (`ssh` or `local`);
  - server ID for SSH terminals;
  - resolved profile ID;
  - whether the terminal inherits the global default profile;
  - an apply callback.
- `Settings -> Terminal Profile -> Apply to open terminals` now:
  - validates the current profile;
  - saves unsaved edits first;
  - applies the backend-returned latest profile to matching open terminals;
  - reports the number of updated terminals.
- Applying a profile updates xterm options in place and schedules the existing
  fit/PTY resize path.
- Applying a profile also updates the terminal host background CSS variable so
  background color changes are visible immediately.
- Font selection is now a preset dropdown plus Custom manual input.
- Added client and backend validation for unsafe font family values.
- Settings header actions are now sticky in the overlay:
  Save, Save and Close, unsaved-change indicator, and close X.
- Ctrl+S saves Settings.
- Esc triggers the existing AppDialogHost close/unsaved-change flow.
- Save and Close is handled by App-level save and closes only after save
  succeeds.

### Safety / Isolation

- Applying a profile does not reconnect SSH.
- Applying a profile does not recreate PTY.
- Applying a profile does not dispose or recreate xterm.
- Applying a profile does not clear terminal buffer.
- Applying a profile does not drop unsubmitted terminal input.
- No terminal input/output/buffer/clipboard content is logged or exported.
- The following were not changed:
  - SSH runtime;
  - Terminal PTY lifecycle;
  - Monitor runtime;
  - SFTP/SCP runtime;
  - Tunnel runtime;
  - Docker runtime;
  - Local Terminal runtime;

## 2026-06-22 交接：Network Details / 监听端口与连接 MVP

### 当前完成

- 已新增“网络详情”大浮窗，首页左侧 Monitor 网络曲线默认打开“端口与连接”Tab。
- Network Diagnostics 已嵌入“网络详情”的“网络诊断”Tab；App 根模板不再渲染独立旧诊断浮窗。
- 顶部菜单入口显示“网络详情”；Dashboard 旧“网络诊断”快捷入口仍进入 Network Details，但默认选中诊断 Tab。
- 后端新增 `internal/networkinspect`，通过独立 SSH exec/context 采集端口监听和连接快照。
- Wails typed API 已新增：
  - `OpenNetworkInspectionContext`
  - `GetNetworkEndpointSnapshot`
  - `CloseNetworkInspectionContext`
- Wails 绑定已重新生成。

### 数据语义

- 优先使用 `ss -H -lntup` 和 `ss -H -tinp state established`。
- fallback 顺序为 `ss` -> `netstat` -> `/proc/net/tcp*` / `/proc/net/udp*`。
- `ss -i` 中明确存在 `bytes_sent` / `bytes_received` 时才显示上传 / 下载累计字节。
- 缺少字节计数时显示 `—`；不得用接口总流量伪装单进程流量，不得把 `bytes_acked` 当成 `bytes_sent`。
- `/proc` fallback 只读取 `/proc/[pid]/comm`，不读取 `/proc/[pid]/cmdline` 或 `/proc/[pid]/environ`。
- runtime 数据只保存在内存，不进入 SQLite、Backup / Export、Command History、Favorites、localStorage 或 Pinia 持久化。

### 安全和生命周期

- Network Details 不复用 SSH terminal session，不调用 `WriteTerminal`，不复用 Monitor / SFTP / Tunnel / Docker / Service Manager SSH client。
- Network Details 失败不调用 `DisconnectServer(serverID)`，不关闭 SSH terminal。
- `DisconnectServer(serverID)` 只取消对应 server 的 Network Inspection context，不影响其他服务器。
- app 退出时会 `StopAll` 清理 Network Inspection contexts。
- app logs 只允许 sanitized 生命周期摘要，不记录原始 socket 输出、远程 IP 列表、cmdline、environ、credential 或 terminal output。

### UI 状态

- 首页网络接口选择框宽度：92px，min 84px，max 104px。
- 首页网络卡片最小高度：178px。
- 首页网络曲线高度：76px。
- 点击网卡 select / 刷新按钮会 stopPropagation，不会误开网络详情。
- Network Details 支持搜索、协议筛选、状态筛选和表头排序；搜索只在已有 snapshot 上进行，不触发远程 SSH 命令。
- 实时刷新默认关闭；开启后 5 秒刷新一次，上一轮未结束会跳过，连续 3 次失败自动关闭。

### 验证基线

- `gofmt`：通过。
- `go vet ./...`：通过。
- `go test ./...`：通过。
- `cmd.exe /c npm run type-check`：通过。
- `cmd.exe /c npm run test -- --run`：通过，53 个测试文件、555 个测试。
- `cmd.exe /c npm run build`：通过。
- `wails build`：Windows amd64 通过。
- `git diff --check`：通过。
- EXE：`build/bin/ServerPilot.exe`
- SHA-256：`161228EE4B33C74E0E8D142D321CB1F2F69894C9895BB24E8CF590030F8A51AE`

### 继续暂缓

- OpenWrt procd `logread`。
- Docker Compose。
- OpenRC / SysV init。
- 抓包、pcap、eBPF、Wireshark。
- 防火墙编辑、连接终止、进程强制结束、端口转发自动创建。
- 网络历史持久化、多服务器聚合端口列表、远程 IP 地理位置、全量连接明细导出。

## 2026-06-20 - 交接：终端 Ctrl+滚轮真实 WebView 后续修复

### 当前结果

用户确认上一版真实手测无效后，本轮已经完成后续修复并跑完验证。影响范围仅限 Ctrl+鼠标滚轮调整终端字体大小。

触发本轮后续修复的用户反馈：

```text
Ctrl+滚轮,手测没有任何效果
```

本轮基线：

```text
30ed32e fix: make terminal ctrl wheel zoom functional
```

新构建：

- 路径：`build/bin/ServerPilot.exe`
- SHA-256：
  `730D524FE525F1C950800FD82F1508E419F96DA9C125751645791AD6E7FA5A60`

### 改动内容

- 应用级修饰键跟踪：
  - `App.vue` 注册 capture 阶段 keydown / keyup listener。
  - window blur 时重置 Ctrl/Meta 跟踪状态。
- WebView guard：
  - `webviewZoomGuard.ts` 通过 `ctrlKey`、`metaKey`、
    `getModifierState('Control')`、`getModifierState('Meta')` 和物理按键跟踪状态识别 Ctrl/Cmd wheel。
  - 继续阻止整个 WebView 页面缩放，并按 `data-terminal-session-id` 派发终端缩放。
- xterm 拦截：
  - `TerminalView.vue` 和 `LocalTerminalView.vue` 注册 `terminal.attachCustomWheelEventHandler`。
  - handler 消费终端缩放手势、阻止默认行为、停止传播、应用当前 session 缩放，并返回 `false`，避免 xterm 把同一事件当普通滚动处理。
- xterm 6 字号应用：
  - `terminalZoom.ts` 直接写 `terminal.options.fontSize`。
  - 如果终端暴露 `refresh()` 和 `rows`，刷新可见行。

### 为什么需要这次后续修复

上一版构建通过了单元测试，但用户真实手测仍无效。最可能的缺口是：旧实现假设 wheel 事件总会携带可靠的 `ctrlKey` / `metaKey`，并且没有使用 xterm 自己的 custom wheel interception hook。本轮明确覆盖了这些真实 WebView / xterm 路径。

  - `src/utils/webviewZoomGuard.test.ts`
  - `src/utils/terminalZoom.test.ts`

## 2026-06-19 - Terminal Profile Create Placement And Connected Server Tab Edit

### Baseline

- Baseline commit before this round:
  `caa33ae0b5623e127eb3a6770d8834a4ec66a2f0`
  (`fix: simplify terminal profile deletion and settings actions`).
- Task scope was limited to Terminal Profile UI placement and SSH
  workspace/terminal tab context menu edit behavior.

### User-Reported Issues

1. Terminal Profile `新建` button was positioned too far away from the Profile
   list.
2. Connected SSH server / terminal tab right-click menu needed an `编辑` action
   to open the server edit dialog directly.

### Implementation

- `SettingsView.vue`
  - Removed the Profile `新建` button from the far section header.
  - Added `.terminal-profile-list-header` inside the left Profile list.
  - Kept the existing `newTerminalProfile()` flow, so clicking `新建` creates an
    unsaved Profile form and selects it in the editor.
- `style.css`
  - Added compact list-header styling that keeps the title and button bound to
    the Profile list and avoids horizontal overflow.
- `WorkspaceTabs.vue`
  - Added typed `editServer` emit.
  - Added `编辑` as the first menu item for SSH terminal and workspace-only
    server tabs.
  - Local Terminal tab menus remain server-edit-free.
  - Selecting any non-local menu item now closes the context menu.
- `App.vue`
  - Added `editServerFromTab(connectionId)` to open the existing
    `ConnectionDialog` for the tab's server.
  - Missing server config shows `无法编辑，该服务器配置不存在。`.
  - `WorkspaceTabs` instances and the existing `TerminalWorkspace`
    `edit-workspace` event now share that edit path.
  - Save detects active runtime state before saving; connected edits now show:
    `服务器配置已保存，当前已连接会话不会自动重连，新配置将在下次连接时生效。`
  - No disconnect, reconnect, SFTP close, monitor close, tunnel close, or
    terminal rebuild is triggered by the edit/save path.
- `stores/terminal.ts`
  - `syncConnectionState` now updates workspace server name and open terminal
    tab titles from the saved connection name.
  - This is display synchronization only; it does not change runtime ownership
    or lifecycle semantics.

- `SettingsView.test.ts`
  - Verifies the Profile `新建` button is in the Profile list header.
  - Verifies the far section header no longer contains that button.
  - Verifies clicking `新建` selects an unsaved new Profile in the editor and
    does not persist immediately.
- `WorkspaceTabs.test.ts`
  - Verifies SSH terminal tab context menus expose `编辑` first.
  - Verifies selecting `编辑` emits `editServer` with the correct server ID.
  - Verifies edit does not emit disconnect/final-disconnect and does not close
    terminal/SFTP sessions.
  - Verifies workspace-only server tabs also emit edit.
  - Verifies Local Terminal tab menus do not contain server edit.
- `App.navigation.test.ts`
  - Verifies the edit/save path for an active server does not call
    `DisconnectServer`.
  - Verifies the edit/save path does not call `OpenTerminal`.
  - Verifies open terminal/workspace labels update from the saved server name.

## 2026-06-19 - Terminal Profile Create Item Ordering And Action Order Polish

### Baseline

- Baseline commit before this round:
  `ed70c2fad079f2092ed21de8129a5e05d2977a04`
  (`fix: align terminal profile create action`).
- Task scope was limited to Settings -> Terminal Profile list item placement
  and editor action button order.

### User-Reported Issues

1. `新建` was still positioned incorrectly because it was fixed at the list
   container bottom, leaving a large blank gap after the last Profile.
2. The requested semantics were: default Profile first, custom Profiles after
   it, and `新建` as the next list item after the last Profile.
3. Editor bottom action order needed to change from:
   `保存 Profile`, `复制`, `设为全局默认`, `应用到已打开终端`
   to:
   `复制`, `设为全局默认`, `应用到已打开终端`, `保存 Profile`.

### Implementation

- `SettingsView.vue`
  - Removed `terminal-profile-list-scroll` and `terminal-profile-list-footer`.
  - Profile rows and the create item now render in the same
    `.terminal-profile-list` flow.
  - `terminal-profile-create-item` is the final list item.
  - The built-in `default` Profile remains sorted first in the Settings UI.
  - Reordered editor actions so `保存 Profile` is last while staying primary.
- `style.css`
  - Converted the Profile list from fixed scroll-body/footer layout to one
    scrolling list flow with `align-content: start`.
  - Added `terminal-profile-create-item` using the same two-column action
    layout as Profile rows.
  - Kept shared `profile-action-button` sizing for `新建` and `删除`.
  - Kept delete danger styling and row label ellipsis behavior.
- `SettingsView.test.ts`
  - Verifies only-default order: default row, then `新建`.
  - Verifies multi-Profile order: default row, custom rows, then `新建`.
  - Verifies saving a new Profile inserts it above `新建`.
  - Verifies action order: `复制`, `设为全局默认`, `应用到已打开终端`, `保存 Profile`.

## 2026-06-19 - Terminal Profile Delete Semantics And Settings Action Style

### Baseline

- Previous commit: `9f65805f9302922f31fa3feb865ce1cd772a1ad9`
  (`fix: improve terminal profile delete and settings header layering`).
- Task type: UI and deletion semantics acceptance fix, not a new feature stage.
- Architecture freeze v1.0 remained in effect.

### What Changed

- Removed the redundant editor action-row `删除` button from Terminal Profile
  settings.
- The only Profile delete entry is now the visible row-level button in the
  Profile list.
- Default Profile and the current global default Profile remain non-deletable.
- Non-default Profiles can now be deleted even if referenced by servers.
- If a Profile is referenced by servers, Settings shows a danger confirmation:
  affected count, detach behavior, and continue/cancel choice.
- On confirmed delete, affected server rows have `terminal_profile_id` cleared
  and therefore inherit the global default Profile.
- Existing open terminals keep their current applied style; no forced reset or
  runtime rebuild occurs.
- Added typed contracts:
  - `DeleteTerminalProfileRequest`
  - `DeleteTerminalProfileResponse`
- Wails API now accepts the typed delete request and returns the typed delete
  response.
- Persistence delete is transactional:
  - reject default/global default Profile;
  - count server usages;
  - if not forced and in use, return `TERMINAL_PROFILE_IN_USE`;
  - if forced, clear server Profile references;
  - delete the Profile.
- Settings emits `terminalProfileDeleted` when server references were detached;
  App refreshes the server list.
- Settings header action styles were swapped:
  - `保存并关闭` is now primary;
  - `保存` is now secondary;
  - close X is unchanged.
- Ctrl+S still saves only.
- Save-and-close semantics remain unchanged.

### Files Changed

- `app.go`
- `internal/domain/terminal_profile.go`
- `internal/persistence/terminal_profiles.go`
- `internal/persistence/store_test.go`
- `frontend/src/App.vue`
- `frontend/src/api/backend.ts`
- `frontend/src/components/SettingsView.vue`
- `frontend/src/components/SettingsView.test.ts`
- `frontend/src/stores/terminalProfiles.ts`
- `frontend/src/types.ts`
- `frontend/wailsjs/go/main/App.d.ts`
- `frontend/wailsjs/go/models.ts`
- `AI_BRIEF.md`
- `DEV_PROGRESS.md`
- `HANDOFF.md`

### Tests Added / Updated

- Store test now verifies:
  - deleting global default Profile is rejected;
  - deleting in-use Profile with `forceDetachServers=false` returns
    `ErrTerminalProfileInUse`;
  - deleting in-use Profile with `forceDetachServers=true` detaches the server;
  - detached server inherits the global default Profile.
- Settings tests now verify:
  - editor bottom delete button is gone;
  - row delete button remains visible;
  - in-use custom Profile delete remains enabled;
  - in-use delete shows server-count confirmation;
  - force delete sends `forceDetachServers=true`;
  - delete success emits server-list refresh event;
  - `保存并关闭` is primary and `保存` is secondary;
  - save and save-and-close logic remains unchanged.
